Birth snapshot only: in-flight orbs never resize (the `dmg: f.dmg` precedent), so the audit's stored `sr` and the rim/swat/cross tests read one constant radius per shot. **No cap** — growth-uncapped is the doctrine, the arena rim is the physical wall (rim death reads `dr + st.r`) and the round reset is the balance bound. *Renderer, three lines:* the halo already inks the hitbox disc at `st.r`, but the nucleus art is delivery-native (a grown orb would read as a small bead inside a big halo), so `drawShot` passes the scale `st.r / row r` into a new optional `ks` parameter on `drawPolySet` (undefined/1 = the identity for every other caller); the orb's art is a 24-gon centred on the local origin, so the scale is centred; ink stays 2px (quiet print — ink never scales). *Authoring:* `brief: '+SPD +SIZE / Hit'` (the ROW INTEGRITY guard requires one the moment a trait carries an axis outside {spin, dmg}), `line` now 'an orb per rev · orbs grow per landing · blades swat orbs'. *New boot guard:* `trait.shotR` must be positive and finite — a negative rate shrinks orbs toward zero and NaN would silently poison every birth radius.
*Sizing was measured, not guessed (60 forced-staff battles — both holders × 5 opponents × 6 seeds, read-only).* Landings per DECIDED round are few: **median 4, p90 8–15, max 15** (staff rounds usually end with the holder beaten down, not with the staff winning); undecided rounds grind to **max 37** at the budget. Swats are the sink: median 2–4 orbs swatted per round (max 14), and the swat test scales with `st.r`, so growth carries its own brake. At 20%: a median round ends ≈ 2.1× orb, ≈ 9 landings reach body size (r 64), and the observed max — **14 consecutive landings — hit 12.8× (r 150.2)**. Holder flavour follows the skill-r29 carrier law: Heavy's own +0.9 dmg/hit kills at ~13–15 landings (his measured max), so his staff grows moderately while his orbs hit harder; Speedy's flat-1 orbs let long rounds escalate endlessly. Unlike the spear, **a rate re-tune is a one-token edit** — no ladder, no re-bake, no gate.
*Proofs (Tier 0 only — no geometry moved).* `.qa/seam-proof.mjs` extended to **17/17** (nine new assertions: the exact birth-radius law over 109 births, per-fighter independence, the round-reset LAW at the roll, the wand untouched at 29/29 sparks, both shotR guards proven to FIRE) · lengrow-build-proof **58/58** · chromecheck **PASS** (legend 812B → 825B on the authored labels; pairing section green) · pair-proof **37/37** (the renderer was touched in drawShot/drawPolySet — the 23-cast-table-read audit stays clean). **Fingerprints: 1 of 8 moved — exactly the staff+hammer s1 cell** (11,704 → 15,465 ticks, `92e44923…` → `4ad73291de285af6c9765e33c6d5efa6`); the other seven are bit-identical and neither natural cell rolls the staff. That is the first time the suite has covered a growth axis (the spear's three builds were invisible to it), and the delta is itself the proof the other 21 weapons did not move. New standing baseline **`.qa/fp-shotgrow-r33.json`** (md5 `daa7971ce5d5693feb16fd0100910684`) — the third deliberate rewrite, after the facing pass and the stall pass; `fp-stall-r25.json` retires to the historical record beside `fp-lead-r22.json`.
*Owed.* The rate is PROVISIONAL — the owner's playtest decides, and a re-tune is one token (the spear's two same-day raises are the precedent for expecting one). The suite's two blind axes are unchanged (it still never crosses two shots and still never rolls a spear) — the 484-cell re-run, the resolver rework and the spear-bearing cell all stay **DEFERRED to the end of the pass**; the staff's numbers in `scratch/baseline-484.csv` are pre-trait. The single-shot birth path got no rim clamp (the volley path's clamp now uses `effR`): an oversized orb born past the rim dies honestly on its birth tick — disclosed trade-off, and a separate decision if the owner ever sees a birth pop he dislikes. **20 weapon traits remain**, then more bots.

**NO COMBOS — `nocombo-r34`: `skill.lenSteps` retired, the spear's cap uniform at 12 for every holder (2026-10-31; the owner approved the full bundle — retirement, chrome fix, wrap guard, codified ruling, docs and a leftover sweep — with *"yes do it all, and clean it up afterwards"*, and on the combo mechanic itself: *"if i ever did that, its a mistake on my side"*. Canonical md5 `9f340f9d9c23c2e6804c1b553b219433` → **`7c51200b05929a7231ea126bd35df84c`**, 3,506 → **3,507 lines**, stamp `shotgrow-r33` → **`nocombo-r34`**.)**
*The ruling, codified.* A skill and a trait may stack **only by addition on a shared axis**; neither may reach into the other's mechanic. No unique fighter×weapon combinations; every skill universal (live on all 22 weapons) or class-independent-contextual, so a bot's identity survives the dice roll by construction. It now lives in three places a builder will read: the canonical's REGISTRY block (the FIGHTERS comment and the WHAT DOES NOT EXIST bullet), "Fighter skill vs weapon trait" above, and the pass order — trait mechanics first (20 remain), then the cast, then balance.
*The retirement.* `LEN_CAP`/`LEN_STEPS_TOP` deleted — every loop, clamp, boot guard and error string reads `LEN_STEPS` (12); the six carrier-era baked rows (steps 13–18) purged from `LEN_CIRCLES`; `REACH.spear` 829.571 → **505.488** (the step-12 top; overhang ~5px against the 500px half-arena, was ~330px); top-step tip growth back to **+200.1%** (tipK 3.0009), the owner-playtest-approved look (*"it looks good now"*); Speedy's row loses `lenSteps: 6` and her label returns to **'+SPD / Hit'**. Rows 1–12 were byte-identical all along (r29's own diff proof), so the purge **removed rows, never remapped them** — asserted by identity in build-proof section O. Retired with the taller ladder: the +37.5% spear-battle sim cost, the unmeasured law-3 band at 829.571, and the carrier-overrule question.
*Chrome.* The VS poster's skill line gains the weapon line's wrap guard — threshold **26** chars at 700 32px (~16px/char ⇒ ~416px inside the ~450px half; the weapon line's 20 is too eager for this font), ' · '-first split with a mid-space fallback, 38px line spacing. The owner's screenshot showed an unwrapped long skill line running across the centre divider into the other half. Both shipped lines (21 and 17 chars) stay single-line, so pair-proof's full-string poster assertions pass unchanged. The legend's short label needed no fix — '+SPD / Hit' fits.
*Proofs (Tier 0 + Tier 1, geometry moved):* build-proof **57/57** — section O rewritten as the uniform-cap proof: no `lenSteps` on any skill object, ladder exactly 13 rows / 12 baked / tipK 13, both bots clamp **12/12** under 60 synthetic hits, real battles saturate **5/5** across BOTH slots and never pass the top, REACH = the step-12 reach, arena overhang disclosed · `lengrow-bake --verify` **12/12** (poke -0.30px, miss 2.30–4.25px) · inscribe GATE PASS · leadproof ALL PASS · flipproof ALL PASS · pair-proof **37/37** (section C's LEN_CAP assert replaced by the no-combos assert; the tripwire now audits **27** identity reads) · seam-proof **17/17** · chromecheck PASS. Fingerprints **0 of 8** against `.qa/fp-shotgrow-r33.json` — for the spear this identity is VACUOUS as before (no spear cell; the suite's blind axes are unchanged), so the file stands as the baseline and no twin was written; the spear-side evidence is build-proof N/O (3/3 divergent, 5/5 saturated).
*Sweep.* The owner's cleanup instruction covered the whole tree: every live reference to the old spear mechanics (`lenSteps`, `LEN_CAP`, `LEN_STEPS_TOP`, 829.571, +420%, '+SPD +LEN', the carrier law) is either deleted or explicitly marked history — in the canonical, the three proof tools that read them (build-proof, pair-proof, lengrow-bake's comment layer; the bake's typeof-guarded probe stays for pre-retirement canonicals and now always falls back to `LEN_STEPS`), and the five live docs. The sigil "carrier" (the one diegetic element wearing the wielder's colour) is a different word and stays.
*Owed:* the deferred bundle is unchanged — 484-cell re-run, law-3 resolver rework, spear-bearing fingerprint cell, all at the END of the skills pass; the kit-route rebuild at the next commission kickoff. The retirement added no new debt; it removed three items from r29's owed list.

**Bind fix — `bindfix-r35`: latched binds push apart from their first tick, and crossed weapons rotate open instead of freezing (2026-10-31; owner playtest, seed 32542 crossbow×staff: *"the crossbow and staff is basically locked up together for a few seconds, this looks very ugly and bad, and should not be possible"*, diagnosis accepted, fix scope C approved. Canonical md5 `7c51200b05929a7231ea126bd35df84c` → **`a1cb90eb2a017d68e33cbf7a4131ef69`**, 3,507 → **3,525 lines**, stamp `nocombo-r34` → **`bindfix-r35`**.)**
*Diagnosis — reproduced headless at the same seed (two 74–75-tick binds back-to-back in round 1), then traced tick by tick.* Two mechanisms losing a force battle in the worst-case geometry, not one bug. (1) The clinch spring's smoothstep ramp (`2200·sm`) only overtakes the press that holds a vertical/rim stack at clinchT ≈ 31 of 48 — for the first ~25 ticks of such a bind the spring loses and the pair reads as a dead lock (bodies clamped at exactly 2·bodyR, speeds ~0). (2) The pin-escape nudge's live torque sign sawtooths in a symmetric X-cross — crossbow bow-arms × a shaft: the deepest contacting circle pair alternates sides tick to tick, measured ±0.046 rad/tick against a spin·dt of 0.046, net rotation ZERO — so the crossed weapons visibly freeze and vibrate instead of orbiting out. Launcher×launcher shows it worst: spine damage is zero by the projectile rule, so no hit recoil or kill ever breaks the pose. Pre-fix frequency: 2/12 forced crossbow+staff cells and 2/20 naturals, 54–75 ticks each.
*The fix — two dials inside the existing seams, stateless.* `CFG.clinchFloor` 1500: while the blade latch is shut the separation acceleration is floored, so a bind pushes itself apart from its FIRST tick; mature binds (sm→1 ⇒ 2200) and the post-release decay are untouched — a force, never an impulse, doctrine intact. And inside a latched bind the pin-escape nudge takes the sign of each weapon's own spin (rotate WITH the orbit, up to twice the orbit rate opens the scissors) instead of the live torque sign; unlatched deep wedges keep the physical torque sign. The first design — latch the torque sign once per episode — was measured and DROPPED: a captured wrong scissor direction held one bind for 384 ticks.
*Chosen by a four-variant sweep, same 32 cells (12 forced crossbow+staff + 20 naturals).* none: 4 binds incl. the owner's seed · floor alone: owner seed cleared but 247/596/384-tick open-space cross-tracking monsters appeared (bodies pushed apart while long weapons keep crossing) · floor + torque-latched nudge: monsters partly remain · **floor + spin-aligned nudge (shipped): 1/12 forced (64t, sawtooth-free) + 2/20 naturals (48t clean; 69t an axe×flail chain wrap — a different texture, pre-existing, and its tail separates textbook-clean: full ramp, d opening ~15px/tick)**. Owner seed and s99 clean; every monster dead.
*Proofs and fallout.* Tier 0: build-proof 57/57 · seam-proof 17/17 · pair-proof 37/37 · chromecheck PASS. pair-proof's two sweep sets needed a SANCTIONED re-seed ([1,4]→[4,21], F's seed 1→7): the bind fix shifted chaotic battle lengths and the old seeds landed in the deliberate stall class (2/4 decided) — the probe's own vacuity guard FAILED them first, exactly as designed; the new seeds were verified to decide on both casts on the landed build. No geometry moved, so no Tier 1. Fingerprints **8 of 8 moved — expected and sanctioned** (every latched bind and every latch changed): new standing baseline `.qa/fp-bindfix-r35.json`. Stall rate flat: 24 naturals, 17 → 19 undecided at the 18,000-tick budget with flips BOTH directions (6 dec→und, 4 und→dec) — chaotic noise on the pre-existing deliberate-stall regime, not a regression.
*Owed:* nothing new. On record as known textures: the axe×flail chain-wrap residue (69t) and the open-space cross-tracking class the floor alone exposed (now suppressed by the spin-aligned nudge, but the geometry exists). The deferred bundle is unchanged.

**The dagger's spin growth lands as `spingrow-r36` — the pass's third weapon trait and its first MELEE one (2026-10-31; owner: *"i just want the dagger to gain rotation speed each hit, so at some point it gets ridicuolously fast"*, rate delegated by his *"you probe and pick"*, the damage question waved off — *"doesnt really matter, we will balance it all later, we are just working on trait mechanics"*. Canonical md5 `a1cb90eb2a017d68e33cbf7a4131ef69` → **`b7b89b34f0b1795c438e1e57006b2727`**, 3,525 → **3,546 lines**, stamp `bindfix-r35` → **`spingrow-r36`**.)**
*Design — the cheapest rung of the ladder: no new consumer, no geometry.* `trait.spin` 0.0 → **2.0 PROVISIONAL** (rad/s per damaging hit), the same axis the launchers' 0.70 fire-rate baseline already runs, consumed by the existing `grow()` line. The class-wide `dmg` 0.7 placeholder stays UNTOUCHED (a balance-pass decision), so the derived legend brief honestly reads `+SPD +DMG / Hit` while the row line teaches the unique trait: 'spins faster per damaging hit'. The trait COMPOUNDS by design — spin is hit cadence (law-4's deep-disengage gate re-arms about once per revolution), so hits arrive faster as the blade speeds up; Speedy's skill.spin 0.30 ADDS on the shared axis (codified stacking; effective 2.3/hit vs Heavy's plain 2.0).
*Rate picked from a measured four-rate ladder* (0/1/2/4; forced dagger×hammer both casts + mirror, 8 seeds, 12,000-tick cap): at 2.0 a decided round ends at **4.4–6.8 rev/s — 8–12× the 0.56 rev/s base** — crosses 4× base at ~12s and stays out of the ≥10 rev/s band where the DRAWN blade wagon-wheels at 60fps (rate 4 lived there in 94% of rounds, and the owner had just closed an "ugly" report; rate 1 peaked at "fast, not ridiculous"). Observed max 42.6 rad/s; `omegaCap` 140 stays a dormant stability ceiling. Disclosed emergent effect: dagger-mirror undecided rounds fell 8/24 → 3/24 at the probe cap — the snowball decides rounds; the deliberate-stall regime itself is unchanged (no timer/ramp/shield touch).
*Proofs.* Tier 0: build-proof 57/57 · seam-proof **22/22** (new section F: the growth law exact on 0/329 off-law hits across 9 forced battles — the first draft's "15 off-law" were t=1 reveal-phase dmg re-anchors, gated out by requiring the tick to ENTER as 'fight' — plus 18 round starts all resetting |spin| to the weapon omega (the LAW), escalation ≥4× base observed, and melee-axis exclusivity static; the events cursor bug it shipped with is recorded in the section's own comments: `G.events` DRAINS per tick, rescan from 0) · pair-proof 37/37 · chromecheck PASS (labels changed: the row line and the derived brief). Fingerprints **1 of 8 moved — exactly the hammer+dagger s5 cell**, the other seven bit-identical (the surgical pattern `shotgrow-r33` set: the moved cell IS the trait, and the identical seven are the neutrality proof for every other rolled pair; neither natural rolled a dagger). New standing baseline `.qa/fp-spingrow-r36.json` (md5 `8279c87272fde990d66516173f148bcc`).
*Owed:* the uniqueness reconciliation when the pass reaches the launchers — whether their 0.70 spin baseline stays class data or gives way to each unique trait. The BOOMERANG is PARKED to the new-trigger rung with a measured geometry note: its loop radius = speed÷turn and period = 2π÷turn, so growing turn kills its reach (a self-defeating nerf) and growing speed flattens its curve toward the shuriken's silhouette — its proper trait must make the loop itself an asset. **[DISCHARGED the same day at `returngrow-r37`: the boomerang trait landed, and the geometry note's warning is exactly what shaped it — speed AND turn grow together so the radius never moves, and the Return makes the loop itself the asset. The launchers' 0.70-baseline reconciliation question still stands.]** The deferred bundle is unchanged.

**The boomerang's Return + cycle growth lands as `returngrow-r37` — the pass's fourth weapon trait and its FIRST CAPPED growth axis (2026-10-31; owner design pick **The Return** from the menu, kept after seeing the measured early-arm nerf — *"No — I still want the Return"* — with the retune delegated to a four-file probe under his benefit test *"each weapon traint should be somewhat beneficial and be more and more ridiculous"*. Canonical md5 `b7b89b34f0b1795c438e1e57006b2727` → **`19e54c884b76e0c635fc17e2f5fc6a9e`**, 3,546 → **3,641 lines**, stamp `spingrow-r36` → **`returngrow-r37`**.)**
*Mechanic — two axes, one fantasy: the loop grows, and the loop comes home.* `trait.cycle` **0.20 PROVISIONAL** per damaging hit through the existing `grow()` (`ab.shotR || ab.cycle`): each landing grows the holder's NEXT throw's speed AND turn ×1.2 **together at birth**, so the loop **radius stays locked at the row's 200px** at every step (speed÷turn invariant — verified 0 off-radius in 49,141 in-flight checks). The **first capped axis**: `CYCLE_STEPS` 10 applied **at read time** (`Math.min(f.shotStep, CYCLE_STEPS)` at the birth site and the substep-budget site), so no new fighter field exists and the round-reset LAW (`shotStep = 0`) covers it free. The cap has a named reason: a faster loop has no physical wall (the rim bounds orbs, the ladder top bounds length) — the uncapped probe showed the runaway (p90 91 landings ≈ 5,800× row speed; the substep budget exploded). `shot.returns` set: **laps 3, refund 2π** at the row's turn 2.6 — only on the **final lap** (`lap >= laps−1`; first circuit needs ≥¾ arc) can the **OWNER** catch the passing loop within bodyR+r; the catch refunds one full revolution to `fireAcc` — cadence ONLY, the one-trigger growth law intact — so the next throw leaves up to 2× sooner. Missed returns die `expire` at the lap wrap (no orphan orbits). New events `shotCatch`/`shotExpire` carry `lap`; **no new `G.stats` keys** — hashes stay surgical. The shot-law comment now records the vs-OWNER catch clause as the owner-blindness exception. Boot guards: `trait.cycle` positive finite; `returns` needs turn > 0, integer laps ≥ 1, finite refund ≥ 0.
*Retune decided by probe, not taste.* The early-arm catch (¾ of the FIRST circuit) measured as a **nerf** — it removes the multi-lap hunting loops from the sky (bh-S decided landings 15 → 10 median); cycle-only (no Return) was the raw volume winner but drops the fantasy the owner picked. The shipped **final-lap + 2π** form is beneficial in EVERY probed config: decided-median landings **bh-S 20 vs canonical 15 · bh-H 15 vs 12 · mirror 25 vs 21**; catches 2–6/round; expiry deaths 1–3/round (the honest price of a missed Return); machine-gunning is impossible — the lap budget bounds the refund rate (the early-arm+2π variant had p90 1,204 catches/round; the landed form saw ≤7 in an 8,000-tick blowout — s4: 194 throws / 45 landings / 5 catches). Against cycle-only, the Return costs bh-S volume (20 vs 27 — the lap budget itself) and WINS the mirror (25 vs 20), bh-H even at 15. All dials one-token: rate 0.20, refund 2π, laps 3, `CYCLE_STEPS` 10.
*Proofs.* Tier 0: boot probe + 4-seed forced smoke (**0 bad radii / 33,165 in-flight checks**; minCatchLap 2 = laps−1; raw step to 45, cap saturates at 6.19×; guards live) · build-proof **57/57** · seam-proof **34/34** (new section G, 12 assertions — row + axis exclusivity; the birth-snapshot law exact over **618 births**, its first draft's 1/618 off-law DIAGNOSED not weakened: a birth sharing a tick with a landing reads `shotStep` before that tick's `grow()`, so the exact within-tick law allows {step−k … step} for k ≤ that tick's landings; radius lock 0/49,141; cap saturation exact at 1.2^10 with raw step 51; final-lap catch + expire fallback; the refund differential against an injected refund-0 twin as a SUITE-level law — Σ 644 vs 596 throws (+8%), 17 catches — per-battle monotonicity proven FALSE and replaced: a refund shifts the fire phase and chaos reshuffles outcomes, 3 more / 1 fewer, the F-law-3 two-way-flip pattern, disclosed; reset LAW; wand untouched; three guards fire) · pair-proof **37/37** · chromecheck **PASS** both sections (labels changed: brief `+SPD +LOOP / Hit` + the row line). Fingerprints **2 of 8 moved — exactly the boomerang-bearing cells**: forced boomerang+shuriken s1, and natural s7 which rolls the boomerang (verified; 17,346 → 15,002 ticks — the Return shortens its holder's rounds), the FIRST natural cell a trait has moved; the other six bit-identical (`pow(x,0)=1`, ×1 mults exact, spread-null adds no keys). New standing baseline **`.qa/fp-returngrow-r37.json`** (md5 `2317ddeb32af6fdd220ccd2d973d220d`).
*Owed:* `measure.mjs`'s 7s replica-departure enumeration needs `catch`/`expire` + `shotCatch`/`shotExpire` before the next owner-sanctioned run (it would miscount boomerang-cell departures otherwise). The which-knock decision still gates the knock seam; shuriken bounce growth is the standing runner-up for the next cheap slot. Owner sign-off on the landed form pending with the full measurement table. The deferred bundle is unchanged.
*Playtest seeds scouted (7 of 100 naturals roll a boomerang):* **seed 28** — round 2 scythe×boomerang, DECIDED at 12,406 ticks (the shortest watch), 2 catches + 5 expires (both Return outcomes visible), the boomerang side wins · **seed 92** — round 2 a NATURAL shuriken×boomerang (the fingerprint cell's texture), 4 catches, decided at 17,900 · **seed 34** — the Return showcase: wand-holder turns boomerang vs a bow, **25 catches / 24 expires**, but undecided at the 18,000 budget (the deliberate projectile-stall regime; watch it for the catches, not the result).

**667 — (2026-11-01) THE BOOMERANG PARKS AT THE OWNER'S CALL · `scratch/boomvars/` PURGED AT HIS ORDER · THE SHURIKEN TRAIT RULED (ricochet budget, per-rim-bounce trigger, uncapped — probe first).** Owner: *"lets park the boomerang for now, im not happy with it, we look at it later. now we foccus on shuriken… it should just bounce off the rim/edge, give me some suggestions."* The landed `halo-r39` STAYS canonical and untouched — the park is a program-state note, not a revert (an r37 rollback was offered and not taken; do not revert unprompted). The whole boomerang probe tree — `scratch/boomvars/`, 39 files: the parked r38 build, the r37/r38 probe zoo, the halo family, the parked seam-proof rewrite — is **DELETED at the owner's order**; the parts salvage was already complete (halo-r39 carries every machine that survived), so the same-day arc now lives only in entries #663–#666.

*The shuriken rulings (owner picks, pre-probe).* **Axis = RICOCHET BUDGET**: the `bounce` count at birth grows — the star survives more wall touches, ceiling = an effectively immortal star that ends only on a body/swat/cross. CHEAP class (row data + the shared counter, no geometry/bake), **zero measure.mjs debt** (bounces are not departures), and the protected identity: the boomerang's pinball direction was rejected in #665 precisely because it belonged to this slot. Post-bounce speed was rated a weaker cousin of the staff's growth; split-on-bounce was rejected on sight (visual noise — what killed r38 at the eye — plus entity multiplication). **Trigger = PER RIM RICOCHET**: every wall touch grows the counter — *"the star learns the walls"* — a self-compounding snowball and the fastest honest route to ridiculous; the second non-damage trigger of the pass (the catch cycle set the precedent). Per-damaging-hit and per-throw were rated weaker (fantasy link / no event link). **Cap = NONE, probe first**: the staff's uncapped `shotR` precedent applies — the round reset is the balance bound and no jam class exists (no singleton gate; every star still dies on first body/swat/cross); `BOUNCE_STEPS` stays a one-token uniform fallback if the probe measures pathological lifetimes. Probe ladder: rates 0.5 / 1.0 / 2.0 plus the no-trait baseline, cells shuriken×hammer both casts + the shuriken mirror, 8 seeds × 12k ticks.

*Implementation note for the landing (trigger purity):* the counter grows at the REFLECTION site (`own.shotStep++` gated on the row's `trait.bounce`), NOT in `grow()` — blade hits must not feed a ricochet trigger. `shotStep` reuse is clean: the shuriken row has no `shotR`/`cycle`, so `grow()` never touches it for shuriken holders, and roundStart already resets the shared counter. Birth becomes `bounce = row.bounce + floor(rate × shotStep)` — additive stacking per doctrine. The row will need an authored `brief` (the boot guard rejects non-derivable trait axes without one) and a new `shotBounce` event kind for the log.

**668 — (2026-11-01) `bouncegrow-r40` LANDED — THE SHURIKEN'S RICOCHET BUDGET: every wall touch teaches the next star one more (the pass's fifth weapon trait, its second uncapped shot axis, and its fewest-move fingerprint: 1 of 8).** The #667 rulings implemented exactly: row `trait: { spin: 0.70, dmg: 0.0, bounce: 1.0 }` + authored `brief: '+SPD +BOUNCE / Rim'` + the new line *"a star per rev · every wall touch teaches the next star one more ricochet"* (label changed → chromecheck re-run). The TRIGGER lives at the reflection site in `shotStep` — on every ricochet the owner's shared `f.shotStep` advances and a new `shotBounce` event is logged — deliberately NOT in `grow()`, so blade hits can never feed a ricochet trigger. BIRTH (both launch sites) reads the budget once: `bounce = shot.bounce base + floor(rate × shotStep)` — additive integers, in-flight stars never change. UNCAPPED: no jam class exists (no singleton gate; a star still dies on the first body/swat/cross), the round reset is the balance bound — the staff `shotR` precedent; `BOUNCE_STEPS` stays an unneeded one-token fallback. Boot guard (6) joins the row-integrity block: a non-positive or NaN rate is a boot error. The `emit()` kinds list in the REGISTRY block was found ROTTEN (missing shotRim/shotCross/shotCatch/roundStart/battleWin) and completed — the new kind forced the audit.

*The probe ladder (owner picked rate 1.0 off these numbers — `scratch/shurvars/`, 8 seeds × 12k, cells shuriken×hammer both cast orders + the mirror):* landings ~2× baseline at EVERY rate (sh-a med 25 → 49/44/50 for 0.5/1.0/2.0; mirror 49 → 76/71/76) — the benefit saturates, so the rate is an aesthetic dial on the tail only: typical star spends ~1.6 of its budget (most are eaten by a body within a couple of wall touches), deep-round budgets med 102/171/379, max 281/304/**1219**, worst tail star alive 1813t/1813t/**2029t** (34s of pinball at 2.0). Rate 1.0 is the exact-integer law: *budget = 1 + every wall touch your stars ever made this round*.

*The battery on the shipped bytes:* build-proof **57/57** · chromecheck **PASS** · pair-proof **37/37** · seam-proof **46/46** with the new section H (ten assertions: row + exclusivity, the second-sighting BIRTH LAW, TRIGGER PURITY per tick — 42,469 ticks, 929 ricochets, 0 off-law deltas, hammer max step 0 — the SPEND law, the reset law, the escalation witness (worst budget 304), the reflection-site-disabled differential (Σ max-step 710 vs 0, off twin still lands 85 hits), two guards). H's first draft FAILED 3 laws and every failure was a HARNESS misclassification, diagnosed by `/tmp/shurdiag.mjs` and fixed without weakening a single assertion: (1) the sid maps were hoisted across battles but `G.shotSid` restarts per `newGame` — cross-contamination; (2) the shared counter resets on the ROLL tick (`rollWeapons`) while the `roundStart` event lands a reveal-phase later (`startFight`) — the delta law exempts roll ticks, disclosed and counted; (3) `fireShots` runs BEFORE `shotStep` within each substep, so a star can ricochet inside its birth tick — the exact per-star invariant (`st.bounce + logged ricochets === true budget`, constant from first sighting) replaced the naive birth snapshot, and the birth law is checked on the second sighting within the documented substep-order ambiguity (563 births, 561 range-checked, 2 died inside their birth tick, 0 off-law).

*Fingerprints: **1 of 8 moved — exactly the `boomerang+shuriken s1` cell** — the fewest-move landing of the pass and the sharpest isolation proof yet: the other seven (both naturals included) are bit-identical.* New standing baseline `.qa/fp-bouncegrow-r40.json` (md5 `e6a451486444f865ece08a93f9511d95`); `fp-halo-r39.json` becomes a historical record. `measure.mjs` edited-not-run per the owner-go rule: a `shotBounce` counter for report visibility (a ricochet is NOT a departure — the accounting laws are untouched), md5 `c58414f2ccda3247262887ee70ae57b4`, 1,393 lines.

*DISCLOSED — the probe-stamp miss (house rule violated, caught pre-landing, fixed with asserted replacements):* the six `scratch/shurvars/` probe files initially carried the canonical's `build halo-r39` stamp — the stamp substitutions targeted strings that do not exist in the canonical (`<title>SPINCLASH — preview</title>`, `window.__build=`) and, unlike every functional edit, they were NOT asserted, so they silently no-oped. All functional edits (row, reflection site, both birth sites) WERE asserted and correct, so every probe number above and the owner's rate-1.0 pick stand on valid behaviour — but a WATCH file opened in the viewer would have identified itself as the canonical. All six files were restamped with asserted replacements (`SHURIKEN PROBE … — NOT canonical` in both the `<title>` and the `#build` node). The lesson is now house law: **stamp substitutions are asserted exactly like functional ones.**

*Hygiene fixed in passing (stale since the halo):* the weapon table's boomerang row still described the r37-era Return (3 laps, `expire`) — rewritten to the landed halo form and marked PARKED; the "Shot flight and deaths" paragraph still said turn 2.6 / ≈200px radius and "the shuriken's single ricochet" — rewritten to the halo oval and the ricochet budget.

*Playtest seeds for the trait:* **natural seed 3** — the no-setup marathon: 432 ricochets, budgets to 433, 74 landings (timeout class, sanctioned by the stall ruling) · **fixed shuriken×hammer (swapped cast) seed 7** — the shortest DECIDED watch, ~135s, budgets to 73 · **fixed shuriken×hammer seed 4** — the pinball storm: budgets to 304, 340 ricochets (timeout class). Watch late-round stars refuse to die at walls — the budget is invisible until a star crosses the arena three, four, five times.

*Owed / state:* **17 weapon traits remain**; the boomerang stays PARKED (landed halo form canonical, revisit at the owner's call); the which-knock decision still gates the knock seam; the deferred bundle (484-cell re-run, law-3 resolver rework, spear cell) is unchanged, still due at the end of the pass.

**669 — (2026-11-01) `ricolaw-r41` LANDED — THE STAR RICOCHET LAW: the rim never kills a star, own stars pass through each other, and only the foe ends a flight. The owner's eye SUPERSEDES `bouncegrow-r40`'s budget axis THE SAME DAY (the pass's second same-day arc after the boomerang's r37→r39); the shuriken's trait slot RE-OPENS — 18 remain.** Owner playtest of the r40 landing, verbatim: *"so i noticed when shurikes collide they destroy each other, i dont like this. and also i realises right now its like 1st shuriken does 1 bounce and then gets destroyed on next or something similar behavior and so on, i ondt like this, cause a viewer will probably think it is a bug, and i dont wanna make it exxplanatory, so change it to they never gets destoryed, only if they hit the opponent or hit their weapon"* — refined from the menu: *"stars only pass through 'own' stars, opponents projectiles/stars will make both 'die'"*. **THE LAW:** a star mirror-reflects off the rim FOREVER at row speed (no budget, no counter, no rim death); SAME-OWNER star pairs skip the cross test (the swarm never self-cancels); enemy shots still cancel both; the flight ends only on the foe's BODY (law-4 hit, consumed), the foe's BLADE (swat), or an ENEMY SHOT (cross). **NO GROWTH AXIS** — the owner picked *"law only, no acceleration"* against a measured per-bounce accel ladder (rate ×1.10/×1.15/×1.20, cap `RICO_STEPS` 10, per-star): acceleration landed harder at every rate (sh-a hits med 126 → 139/153/**173**, and ×1.20 cut the law-only tail — 70-second constant-speed cruise stars, worstLife 4183t — to ~1400t by making stars find a target 3× sooner) but he took the constant-speed law; the ladder numbers stay on record here and in the probe family. Retired with r40: `trait.bounce` + guard (6) + the reflection-site `shotStep++` trigger + the budget half of the birth formula + the authored brief (the row is back to the derived '+SPD / Hit'). SURVIVING from r40: the `shotBounce` event kind (now the law's only log witness), measure.mjs's counter, and the completed emit-kinds list. `bounce` survives in the birth spread as a FROZEN HASH FIELD (fingerprint.mjs hashes shots by an explicit field list including `s.bounce` — dropping the key would move all 8 cells; it is 0 for every row now, disclosed).

*The probe (law + ladder, 8 seeds × 12k, `scratch/shurvars/star-*.html`, all PROBE-stamped with ASSERTED replacements — the r40 stamp-miss lesson applied):* the density IS the spectacle — peaks of ~50–66 stars in flight in shuriken-heavy cells (avg ~9), the defender's game becomes swatting (~1,000 swats per 8-battle sh-a cell), and **perf is flat** (0.56–0.60 ms/tick across all variants — own pairs leave the O(n²) cross loop entirely). Mirror crosses (enemy annihilation) run ~900+ per 8-battle cell. Disclosed consequence of the law: shuriken-heavy rounds decide LESS (sh-a 1–3/8, mirror 1–4/8 — symmetric chip races run to the timeout class the stall ruling sanctions); the law-only worst star cruises 70 seconds.

*The battery on the shipped bytes:* build-proof **57/57** · chromecheck **PASS** (line label changed: *"a star per rev · ricochets forever — only the foe, their blade or shot ends it"*) · pair-proof **37/37** · seam-proof **45/45** — section H REWRITTEN from the budget laws to the ricochet law (9 assertions): the row gate + full budget retirement, exclusivity, THE RIM NEVER KILLS A STAR (2,804 ricochets logged, 0 star rim deaths), THE SPEED LOCK (390,735 in-flight checks, 0 off-speed — the constant-speed law he picked), DEPARTURE ACCOUNTING (1,141 stars born, 0 silent vanishings — immortality must not leak), immortality-is-not-invincibility (492 body hits, 418 swats), enemy crosses alive (408 mutual annihiliations in the mirror), THE SAME-OWNER PASS-THROUGH IS LIVE (7,838 overlap ticks / 23,807 pair-ticks observed with 0 own-pair crosses — pre-ruling every one of those pairs annihilated, the exact bug-look he cut), and law-1 isolation (wand sparks still die at the rim, no ricochet state). One first-draft harness bug fixed and disclosed: the departure-accounting assertion referenced a per-battle map after its loop (`born is not defined`) — hoisted to a counter, no assertion weakened.

*Playtest seeds for the law:* **natural seed 3** — the no-setup spectacle: 853 ricochets, peak 62 stars in flight, 129 landings (timeout class) · **fixed shuriken×hammer (swapped cast) seed 4** — the shortest DECIDED watch, ~129s, the law readable at low density · **fixed shuriken×hammer (default cast) seed 4** — the Speedy swarm: 1,031 ricochets, peak 60 stars, 154 swats (timeout class).

*Owed / state:* **18 weapon traits remain** (the shuriken's slot re-opened by this ruling — its LAW is landed, its growth axis is not; a future trait for the shuriken must build on the ricochet law, not resurrect the budget); the boomerang stays PARKED; the which-knock decision still gates the knock seam; the deferred bundle (484-cell re-run, law-3 resolver rework, spear cell) is unchanged, due at the end of the pass.

**670 — (2026-11-01) THE SHURIKEN'S SLOT CLOSES AT THE OWNER'S WORD — THE LAW IS THE TRAIT. 17 remain.** Owner, asked whether the star needs a growth axis at all: *"yes shuriken is done, and it is very overpowered right now, but that doesnt matter, we are designing mechanics, later we will adjust them and balance the game out."* The ruling that closes it: the 2026-10-31 program law says every weapon gets a unique TRAIT — it does not say the trait must be a growth NUMBER. `shot.ricochet` IS the shuriken's trait: unique (no other row has it), beneficial (5× the pre-trait baseline landings, med 25 → 126), ridiculous (peaks of ~50–66 immortal stars, the defender swatting ~1,000 times per 8-battle cell), and readable with zero explanation — the exact bar the r41 ruling set. **DOCTRINE NOTE (recorded with the close): a weapon's unique trait may be a LAW, not a growth axis** — which widens the design space for the remaining 17 toward flight laws and interaction rules (cheap class, maximally readable) where a number would be weaker. The star's escalation curve already exists on the SHARED axis: the launcher-class `spin: 0.70` grows the wielder's revs per damaging hit, so output climbs within a round and the swarm thickens on its own — density IS the growth. Against adding a bespoke axis: it would stack on the strongest weapon in the game, and the r40→r41 same-day arc is the fresh receipt for what un-inventing a mechanic costs.

*The overpowered disclosure is now BALANCE-PASS MATERIAL, recorded at the owner's own words (verbatim above):* the levers, when the time comes, are numbers — launch cadence, star size, the row's `spin: 0.70`, the timeout pull (decided 1–3/8 in the asymmetric cells) — NOT the law. Nothing is adjusted now, per the standing *"dont think about balance etc and numbers"* ruling.

*The boomerang parallel was noted to the owner and NOT taken up (it stays parked):* the same frame could eventually resolve it — the halo IS its unique mechanic, and whether the `cycle` growth axis stays, goes, or changes is the open question of the revisit. No action.

*Canonical comment-only true-up (the row block and the registry entry still said "slot RE-OPEN, 18 remain"):* corrected to CLOSED/DONE with the doctrine note. Behaviour-identical by proof — fingerprints **8/8 vs the standing `.qa/fp-ricolaw-r41.json`**, NO stamp bump (the registry-block precedent: comment-only edits keep the stamp), md5 moves `bd1b75013a789250bab57ea71e2b52d1` → **`14165e25f96f2a882bb3642ff5d35bfb`**, 3,760 lines, `#build` and `<title>` still read `ricolaw-r41`.

**672 — (2026-11-01) `multgrow-r42` LANDED — THE BOW'S RAIN: every damaging hit thickens the launch cadence, and the bow's own rotation paints the spread. It lands WITH THE GENERALIZED OWNER-BLIND CROSS LAW — no same-owner shot cross anywhere, friendly fire eliminated game-wide. The pass's sixth weapon trait, the launcher class's first growth axis, and the seam-r30 cadence multiplier's FIRST LIVE CARRIER — 16 remain.**

*The road here (recorded because it set doctrine):* with the shuriken closed, the launcher class stood at seven blank clones. The crossbow bolt-breaker proposal died on the owner's **two-bar test** — a trait must be readable by a viewer in ~2 seconds without explanation AND matter against every opponent, melee and ranged (*"hard to catch for the viewer"* + *"what against melee weapons, the crossbow doesnt have any bonus then, so i think its a bit weak idea"*). The fresh menu (siege cannon / knockback king / tome blizzard / bow volley) drew *"elaborate more on the bow"*, and the volley proposal — wake the mult seam with a `trait.mult` growth rate, additive on the seam's axis per the stacking doctrine; rotation paints the spread, so the bow is an accelerating STREAM while the knives' `shot.volley` keeps the static-fan identity; house-default per-damaging-hit trigger; legend '+SPD +RAIN / Hit' — got *"ok go implement it"*.

*The self-eating pathology (the probe ladder's find):* the first ladder (uncapped, rates 0.25/0.5/1.0) exposed a structural wall — cadence growth makes consecutive launches overlap at birth, and the shot-vs-shot cross test still tested SAME-OWNER pairs (the sim's last friendly-fire holdout; the r41 star exemption was the only precedent for skipping them). The volley annihilated itself: rate 1.0 burned 71,758 friendly crosses out of 79,815 fires (~90%), and the benefit plateaued (hits med ~100 at rate 0.5, no better at 1.0). The fork sent to the owner — **A:** generalize owner-blindness to all shot-vs-shot + a uniform cap (recommended) / **B:** cap only, keep the cross rule / **C:** fan growth (flagged: collides with the knives' identity) — came back *"a, do that"*.

*The re-probed grid (fixed law + `MULT_STEPS` 10, 8 seeds × 12k ticks per cell, `scratch/bowvars/`, asserted stamps):* base (law only) is invisibly close to canonical (fires 580/558/1,285 identical; the bb mirror's 2 enemy crosses survive — the isolation proof that the law changes nothing at base cadence). Rate 0.25 → smul cap 3.5: bb hits med 13→25, but bw-a stays at 5 (fails the matters-vs-melee bar). Rate 0.50 → cap 6: **bw-a hits med 5→38 (7.6×)**, bb 13→36, arrows peak med 23 / max 59, perf 0.67 ms/tick. Rate 1.00 → cap 11: bw-a hits med 102 (20×), peak med **106** / max 118 arrows — the firehose, denser than anything the game has shown (the star ceiling was ~66), perf still fine (0.75 ms/tick) but the screen busy. Same-owner crosses: 0 at every rate (by construction). **Owner picked 0.50** from the menu.

*The landing (canonical edits):* the cross loop's star exemption generalizes to `if (A.o === B.o) continue;` (r41's ruling cited in the comment as subsumed) · `MULT_STEPS = 10` beside `CYCLE_STEPS` (read-time cap — cadence has no physical wall, unlike shotR which the rim bounds) · the smul growth term at the launch site · `grow()`'s shared-counter condition extended with `ab.mult` · guard (6): `trait.mult` positive and finite (+ guard-doc item (2) updated — the seam's static side is still declared by nobody) · the bow row (`trait.mult: 0.5`, brief, line, the ruling-record comment) · the registry's new `trait.mult` axis entry + the `shot.mult` entry rewrite · both law bullets (cross = enemy-only; owner-blindness now covers shots) · the reset comment (the shared counter serves rain too).

*The battery on the shipped bytes:* seam-proof **55/55** (was 45): section B REWRITTEN — the static side stays inert (no row `shot.mult`, no fighter `skill.shotMult`) while the bow is proven the sole `trait.mult` holder at 0.5 with `MULT_STEPS` 10; section C's declared-factor laws (1→2×, 3→4×) now run on a GROWTH-OFF twin through the replace-loader — the live rain term would compress the ratio and falsify the law's premise; section D gains the two rain guards (negative, NaN); NEW SECTION I (7 laws): the SOURCE law (generalized line present, star-only line gone), the CADENCE BAND law (accumulator conservation per tick — every one of **4,014 fires** consumed a rotation inside [TAU/6, TAU/smul(step)]), CAP SATURATION (**3,019 saturated fires at EXACTLY TAU/6**, 0 off-ceiling), the GROWTH-OFF TWIN differential (fires 4,161 vs 266 = **15.64×**, hits 295 vs 18, strict on all 4 seeds), FRIENDLY FIRE IS GONE (**0 same-owner cross pairs** across bow mirror + bow-vs-melee + star mirror, 97 enemy pairs alive — the law is not vacuous), the ROUND-RESET law, and the FAN IDENTITY (knives' 3 anchors untouched). Three first-draft harness findings, all diagnosed not weakened: (i) 11 band violations were roundStart-tick `fireAcc` resets (the round-start reset at canonical :1692 zeroes it — the E-section roll-tick lesson reborn; exempt like roll ticks → 4 remained); (ii) the 4 residue were the PIN-ESCAPE NUDGE (canonical :2292 rotates `wAngle` outside the accumulator ledger during stalled binds — all four carried the identical ±0.0912 rad signature; exempt via the observable `f.nudgeTick === G.t`); (iii) I6's bb mirror never reaches round 2 (its rounds stall forever — the deliberate-stall law), so the reset law pools bow×hammer seeds where the hammer closes rounds. H6 annotated: the star pass-through is now an INSTANCE of the general law. Build-proof **57/57** · pair-proof **37/37** · chromecheck **PASS** (row, line and legend changed).

*Fingerprints: **2 of 8 moved — exactly the two bow cells** — and both flipped from 18000-tick timeout to DECIDED (bow+crossbow s2 → 17391, wand+bow s3 → 12418): the rain can now close rounds.* The other six are bit-identical, both naturals included. New standing baseline `.qa/fp-multgrow-r42.json` (md5 `ba86790e5b3bad73098d5220b61114b4`); `fp-ricolaw-r41.json` becomes a historical record. `measure.mjs` needed NO edit — the 4s replica is event-driven, so its `shotCross` counts read the enemy-only law directly (md5 unchanged, `f11a94b5e1ebe56a67132f6dca60cad6`, 1,393 lines — not run per the owner-go rule). The bow+crossbow cell now takes 55.6s to fingerprint, the heaviest in suite history — disclosed, and still Tier-0-cheap.

*Disclosures (balance-pass material by the owner's standing ruling):* the growth is SUPERLINEAR — rain feeds on its own hits (the seam-C superlinearity note now compounds): 7.6× hits vs melee at rate 0.5, 20× at 1.0; density peaks med 23 / max 59 arrows at the landed rate; bow-bearing cells can now decide where they previously stalled; the cross-law change is game-wide — ANY future same-owner shot interaction now passes through by default, which is the owner's chosen reading ("a viewer will probably think it is a bug" applied to every class, not just stars).

*Seeds for the owner's eyes:* **natural s16** (bow+sword — the two-bar case in the wild: full ladder climb, maxStep 100, fires 1,222, hits 100, wins 1-0 at timeout) · **bw-b s3** (DECIDED@11099, wins 2-0 — the rain closes the battle) · **bb s2** (the mirror: fires 1,911, hits 103, peak 54 arrows, maxStep 100, enemy crosses alive). Probe family kept at `scratch/bowvars/` (4 files — base + the three rates under the fixed law; the first-generation pre-law files wore the same names and were rebuilt over, their numbers recorded above — asserted PROBE stamps — the owner picks by eye + numbers; purged at his word the same day once the slot closed — #673, the shurvars precedent).

**Traits: 6 of 22 landed** (spear `len`, staff `shotR`, dagger `spin`, boomerang halo `cycle` PARKED, shuriken LAW closed, bow `mult`) — **16 remain**. Next: the launcher class still holds six blanks (crossbow, wand, knives, javelin-thrower, tome, scroll); the two-bar test is the gate; the knock seam stays queued behind the owner's which-knock decision.

**673 — (2026-11-01) THE BOW'S SLOT CLOSES FOR NOW at the owner's word — *"i think bow is done for now mechanically, we can always easily balance it later right? remove the bow folder if yes"* — and `scratch/bowvars/` is purged. Traits: 6 of 22 landed, 16 remain.** The balance answer, recorded because it is the ruling's premise and it is YES: every strength knob on the rain is a NUMBER, not mechanics — `trait.mult` 0.5 (the rate, one row field), `MULT_STEPS` 10 (the cap; smul ceiling = 1 + rate × cap, one constant), the launcher-class `spin` 0.70 (how fast cadence compounds with rain, row field), and the arrow's speed 620 / dmg / r 5 / len 30 (row fields). Each re-probes in minutes with the standing harness patterns, and the round-reset LAW bounds every round's climb regardless of the numbers. The only non-number piece of the landing is the generalized owner-blind cross law — a game-wide LAW the owner chose deliberately (option A), not a bow dial. The bow joins the shuriken as a closed slot pending the balance pass (doctrine #670 applies: mechanics first, *"later we will adjust them and balance the game out"*). **PURGED:** `scratch/bowvars/` — all 4 probe files (`bow-base.html` law-only, `bow-m25/m50/m100.html` — rates 0.25/0.5/1.0 under the fixed law, cap 10); every number they produced is permanent in #672 (both ladder generations: the self-eating pathology table and the re-probed grid), whose `scratch/bowvars/` pointer stays as a dated record (the #671 dangling-pointer precedent). The canonical is untouched by the purge: md5 `81b4a37e8ff74b332f0fa8d3c35d54b5` verified after. **674 — (2026-11-01) PROCESS RULING: MECHANICS ONLY, AND FAST — the heavy test loop is retired at the owner's order.** Verbatim: *"so from now on, we focus on implementing skill mechanincs/traits for each weapon, nothing elese, no balancing etc. which means you should not test for balancing and the other redundant stuff similar to chat, it does not help/change the mechanics. the reason i tell you this is that it takes so long before you answer back as you have to run all these tests all the time"* — with the time budget confirmed: *"if it is not too lon it is ok, it is just that the last few weapon trait prompts took like over an hour each, this is not doable."* **THE NEW LANDING LOOP (house law from here):** (1) proposal turns run NOTHING; (2) landings = asserted-anchor edits + WATCH (seconds — typo-catching, not testing); (3) ONE smoke run (~30s): boot, forced battle, assert the new mechanic fires and nothing crashes — the owner sanctioned this much runtime; (4) SHARED-CODE landings (laws, seams, the resolver — anything that can silently break other weapons, which is mechanics, not balance) get flagged and run ONLY the single relevant proof section (~30–60s), never the suite; (5) docs stay (zero runtime) with trimmed entries. **BANNED BY DEFAULT:** fingerprint runs (the standing baseline `.qa/fp-multgrow-r42.json` freezes as a record until the owner sanctions a re-run), full battery, numeric probe ladders with combat-outcome stats (hits/decided/win rates), seed-recon tables, balance disclosures, `measure.mjs` (the explicit-go law unchanged). Probe variant files may still be BUILT as eye-families (seconds, asserted PROBE stamps, handed to the owner in the viewer — he picks by eye); counters run only on his explicit ask for a specific number, and then lean (2–3 seeds, density/visibility metrics only, no combat outcomes). The `.qa/` instruments all stay on disk as records; the battery counts in entries through #672 are history. For scale: the bow landing (#672) spent ~35–40 minutes of runs (two ladders ~20 min, full battery ~8 min, seed recon ~8 min) — under this law the same landing costs ~2–3 minutes.

**675 — (2026-11-01) `fan-r43` LANDED — THE KNIVES' GROWING FAN: every damaging hit adds one fan tier, and each tier adds one knife per side — 3 → 13 knives, half-spread 22° → 67°, in quantized visible steps. The pass's seventh weapon trait and the FIRST LANDING UNDER THE #674 FAST PROCESS LAW — 15 remain.** The owner picked from the three-launcher menu (*"the knives sounds good, try and do that first"*), was handed the rate-0.5 probe file in the viewer, and landed it by eye: *"it looks good already implement it and remove the knife folder."* **THE MECHANIC:** `trait.fan` 0.5 on the shared `shotStep` counter (house damaging-hit trigger — landed knives and the holder's blade hits both feed it), consumed per volley by `grownVolley()` at the launch site: tier t = min(floor(shotStep × rate), `FAN_STEPS` 5), each tier k adding one knife per side from that side's outer anchor at ±(22 + k×`FAN_SPREAD` 9)°. The row's static 3-anchor volley is the tier-0 identity and is never mutated; rows without `trait.fan` get the row array itself back — bit-identical by construction, and the volley branch is knives-only, so this is smoke-class, NOT a shared-code landing under 674(4). **THE LOOP, at the new speed:** proposal ran nothing; the eye-family (`scratch/knifevars/` — rates 0.25/0.5/1.0, asserted PROBE stamps) built in seconds; ONE smoke on the probe (~3s: 23 volleys on-formula, mirror legal, guard fires); landing edits + ONE smoke on the shipped bytes (~17s: 279 volleys across knives×hammer and the mirror, **0 off-formula**, max volley exactly 13 at the cap, maxStep 100 proving the counter ran past it, bow and staff cells still firing untouched, the NaN guard throws); `scratch/knifevars/` purged at the owner's word — its numbers are permanent here and in #674's record (the dangling-pointer precedent). **EDITS:** constants (`FAN_STEPS`/`FAN_SPREAD`), the `grownVolley` helper, the launch loop, grow()'s condition, guard (7) + its doc entry, the row (trait.fan 0.5, brief '+SPD +FAN / Hit', line, ruling comment), the registry's `trait.fan` axis entry. **Fingerprints NOT run per #674** — the standing baseline stays frozen at `.qa/fp-multgrow-r42.json`; a future sanctioned re-run should expect knives-bearing cells to move (the suite's forced cells never roll knives; the naturals are unverified). Balance disclosures (the fan compounds with the class spin axis; 13-knife volleys every three revs) are balance-pass material by the standing ruling. Canonical md5 `81b4a37e8ff74b332f0fa8d3c35d54b5` → **`e2803b8a841a0f713467ad802a43f85d`**, **3,836 lines**, stamps `fan-r43`. Total machine time for this landing: under a minute — the #674 law works.

**676 — (2026-11-01) `pinlaw-r44` LANDED — THE CROSSBOW'S NAILS: a bolt that reaches the rim is pinned there like a nail, and the foe who touches it bleeds; the growth is the cadence — the nails arrive faster. Law 1's third row-owned exception and the mult seam's second carrier. The pass's eighth weapon trait — 14 remain.** The owner proposed it whole: *"for the crossbow, i was thinking its bolts stays pinned on the rim/edge of the arena, like nails, and when the opponents touch them, they get hit, the growth is that the crossbow will shoot faster"* — and ruled the four design points: consumed on touch (*"yes consume it"*), cadence growth (*"yes i meant that"*), UNCAPPED (*"exactly no cap, we are just doing mechanics, no balancing, it sounds very overpowerede right now, but it doesnt matter"*), and the legend text delegated (*"just type what you want or leave it, im gonna have a look at the legend and all the text anyways once we are done with all the weapons etc"* → brief '+SPD +NAILS / Hit', line "a bolt per rev · misses NAIL the rim — touch them and bleed").

**THE LAW:** at the rim, a `shot.pin` bolt is NAILED instead of dying — snapped just inside the wall (the ricochet's snap formula, so it can never re-trigger the rim test), `speed` 0, `vx/vy` the unit INWARD normal (which serves double duty: drawArrow's facing AND the law-4 knock direction — an impaled foe is slammed inward, off the wall). A nail is a SHOT AT REST and reuses every existing resolution: the foe's body touch is the standard consumed law-4 hit (and grows the crossbow's cadence through the normal `grow(owner)` path — the nails feed themselves), the foe's blade swats it off, an in-flight enemy shot crosses it out, the roll clears it; the OWNER walks through his own nails (owner-blindness, free since multgrow-r42). A pin is NOT a departure (the ricochet/catch precedent); `'shotPin'` joins the emit-kinds list. The growth half needed ZERO new code: the crossbow row just declares `trait.mult` 0.5 PROVISIONAL and rides the seam exactly as proven for the bow (seam section I — cadence band, cap saturation at smul 6, reset law). Guard (8): `shot.pin` is a boolean (no number to poison, the ricochet precedent) but guarded EXCLUSIVE against `shot.ricochet`/`shot.returns` — a row may own exactly one rim-law exception.

**THREE SMOKE-FOUND ENGINEERING REFINEMENTS (disclosed):** (i) **NAILS DO NOT FIGHT NAILS** — pinned×pinned pairs are exempt from the cross test: statics can never visibly resolve, and two enemy bolts pinned side by side annihilating is exactly the bug-look the owner buried at r41. (ii) **`speed = 0` at the pin** — the substep budget's worst-pair term reads `st.speed`; without this, 400 static nails billed 860+860 px/s of relative travel per tick and inflated the substep count (~5×); tunnel safety is untouched (a bolt approaching a nail budgets from the BOLT's own speed). (iii) **Tail partition + outer break in the cross loop** — nails partition to the tail of the compacted window and the outer loop breaks at the first nail (nail×nail is exempt by law, flier×nail is covered by the flier passes): O(n²) → O(fliers×n) at 444 nails. The partition is a NO-OP when nothing is pinned — every non-crossbow cell keeps its exact shot order, bit-identical. A proposal correction also disclosed: the analysis turn said the boomerang would pass through nails; the landed law applies the standard enemy cross (a halo crossing an enemy nail ends both) — no special case, consistent with the cross law as it stands.

**THE SMOKE (the only runs, ~45s + chromecheck):** xbow×hammer s1 12k ticks — 848 pins, **0 bolt rim deaths**, 625 nail body-hits and 1,483 nail swats (first pass), **0 leaks** across 6,244 born bolts (departure accounting exact), max 444 nails at once; the mirror **DECIDED at 7,146 ticks** with 22 nail-involved crosses (bolts shooting nails off the wall — the counterplay is alive); wand isolation clean (16 spark rim deaths, 0 pin state); the pin+ricochet guard throws; chromecheck **PASS** (row/legend changed). Perf disclosed: the extreme 444-nail timeout battle runs 2.93 ms/tick (playable in the 16.7ms frame budget; the residual cost is the per-shot blade-circle recompute — balance-pass polish, noted not touched), the realistic mirror 1.25. **Fingerprints NOT run per #674** — the baseline stays frozen at `.qa/fp-multgrow-r42.json`; a sanctioned re-run should expect the bow+crossbow s2 cell (and any natural rolling the crossbow) to move. `measure.mjs` comment-aligned (a `shotPin` counter, six sites, "not a departure" semantics like `shotBounce`) — edited not run per the owner-go rule. A seam-proof **section J (the pin law) is DEFERRED** to the next full-battery milestone per #674 — the smoke above is the landing proof; the deferred bundle (484-cell re-run, spear cell, law-3 resolver rework) grows by one item. Canonical md5 `e2803b8a841a0f713467ad802a43f85d` → **`7d38da62efe3a5d826b5f30f993d5b5b`**, **3,887 lines**, stamps `pinlaw-r44`. Emergent, disclosed: long rim-stalls erode as nails accumulate near the wall (feature per the analysis turn; the deliberate-stall regime keeps its late-round teeth). **Traits: 8 of 22 — 14 remain**; launchers left: wand, javelin, tome, scroll.

**677 — (2026-11-01) `pinfix-r45` LANDED — THE OWNER'S PLAYTEST EYE-FIX ON THE NAILS: pinned bolts keep their impact flight line — no flip at the rim. Overpoweredness ruled OUT OF SCOPE.** The owner playtested the crossbow and ruled twice: *"crossbow seems super overpowered, but that does not matter, its not what we are here for"* — the uncapped nail growth stands exactly as landed (#676); balance is the balance pass's business, not this one. And: *"also the bolts flip around once they hit the edge/rim, dont do that."*

**THE DIAGNOSIS + FIX:** the pin site had been setting `st.vx/vy` to the INWARD rim normal — that is the knock direction, but the drawn bolt reads its facing from its velocity, so every pinned bolt visibly spun ~180° at impact. `pinfix-r45` freezes `vx/vy` exactly as the impact flight line (head into the wall, shaft pointing back the way it came — zero rotation at the pin) and stores the knock vector separately as new `st.pnx/st.pny` fields; the nail's impale knock in `shotHitBody` reads `pinned ? (pnx,pny) : (vx,vy)`, so an impaled foe is still shoved off the wall along the inward normal. `pnx/pny` are not in the fingerprint field list → zero hash impact. One edit site + one read site + the registry line; stamps → `pinfix-r45`.

**THE SMOKE (the only runs, ~50s × 2 passes, xbow×hammer s1/s2 × 9,000 ticks):** 1,306 pins — **the NO-FLIP LAW: velocity at pin bit-identical to the last in-flight value on 1,306/1,306 pins** (66 first-pass flags diagnosed as same-tick fire+pin snapshot artifacts — born and pinned inside one tick, legal; true flips 0). Nail knock inward 116/116. The r44 regressions hold: 0 rim deaths, 0 shot leaks (1,536 bolts born), max 444 nails live, mirror still DECIDED. Chromecheck skipped (Tier-0: zero label/legend/row changes). Fingerprints NOT run per #674; the baseline stays frozen at r42. Canonical md5 `3a1f899482a975e623f4a4445248c581` (the session record's `pinlaw-r44` state at the owner's report — post-#676 registry/guard additions inside the same stamp) → **`5e93f7496cd72ffa8e2c06310a9975b2`**, **3,891 lines**, stamps `pinfix-r45`. **Traits: 8 of 22 — 14 remain**; the crossbow is DONE (nails + rate, visuals fixed). Next weapon at the owner's word (launchers left: wand, javelin, tome, scroll).

**678 — (2026-11-01) `skewer-r46` LANDED — THE JAVELIN'S SKEWER: the hit does not consume the spear — it impales, DRAGS the foe to the rim, PINS them there BLEEDING, and their own slowly-orbiting blade cutting the spear is the escape. Law 4's fourth row-owned exception; THE LAW IS THE TRAIT; the growth stays the class fire-rate baseline. The pass's ninth weapon trait — 13 remain.** The owner proposed it in three moves: a true-throw idea first (the weapon vanishing while the spear flies) — WITHDRAWN the same turn (*"the javelin never vanishes from the body, i have changed my mind. it looks a bit confusing for the viewer i think"*); then the skewer (*"when a javelin hits the opponent, it drags the opponent to the rim, pinning them to the rim, and dealing damage like bleeding effect"* + the initial-damage ruling *"the opponent wil take taht initial damage, and as it gets carried through the arena it will also bleed"*); then the escape (*"once the spear carries the opponent, i want the opponents weapon to spin slower, but once their weapon hits the javelin, they are free again"*). Carry bleed is the owner's EYE-JUDGMENT item (*"im not completelyy sol on it… my eyes need to see it first"* — pin bleed confirmed 100%); implementation choices were delegated (*"just do what you think is best, my eyes will judge, dont test it with numbers etc as it takes too long"*).

**THE MECHANIC:** body hit → the normal law-4 damage lands (hitstop, hit flash, the thrower's grow() fires — the class `spin 0.70`) but the spear is NOT consumed: `startSkewer` drops the victim's spin to the `SKEWER.spin` 1.5 rad/s crawl AT THE HIT (sign kept — leadFlip reads it; `spinBase` saved, `grow()` mirrors impaled growth onto it, release restores it EXACTLY), tears out of any latch, and `skewStep` CARRIES the body along the flight line at 1100 px/s (under the chaos ceiling; the velocity is written to `f.vel` so the substep budget sees the honest drag) to the boundary's own clamp radius. At rim arrival the PIN reuses the NAIL machinery (pinned, `speed` 0, snapped just inside, `pnx/pny` — pinfix-r45's facing law rides along: vx/vy stay the impact flight line, so the drawn spear impales the victim head-into-wall). `skewBleed` pulses HP every 26 ticks (2 hp/pulse, pool 100) during the carry (behind `SKEWER.carryBleed`, the eye-judgment toggle — one constant kills carry bleed alone) and unconditionally during the pin; pulses wear the hit FLASH but NOT the hitstop (freezing every 0.43s would stutter the struggle). **THE CUT-FREE:** the victim's blade touching the anchored spear is the escape — the physical circle-swat is the literal truth (tangential chords sweep a bodyR ring that reaches the anchor ~20px inside the victim's centre when the blade faces the wall), and the CUT-FREE WINDOW is the universal guarantee for all 22 rows: blade direction within ±acos(0.75) ≈ ±41° of the spear's outward radial cuts it, armed only after the blade has left the window once (no instant escapes at arrival). `killShot`'s `releaseSkew` hook frees on ANY destruction of the anchor — the cut (the swat event names the victim as the swatter), an enemy cross (the victim's OWN shot shooting it out is emergent), the roll. NO PIN CLOCK: the crawl is the timer; the mechanic is self-terminating. Release pops the victim 160 px/s inward. A killing hit consumes normally (no corpse is dragged); an already-skewered foe takes the plain hit. Guard (9): `shot.skewer` kind-locked to 'javelin', exclusive with every rim-law exception; the SKEWER constants validate at their const block. Events: 'skewer', 'skewerPin', 'bleedTick', 'skewerFree' (carries the sid). Legend authored: brief '+SPD +SKEWER / Hit', line "a spear per rev · the hit SKEWERS — dragged to the rim, pinned, bleeding · your blade cuts free".

**THE SMOKE (the only runs, 3s) + chromecheck:** javelin×hammer s1 + javelin×bow s2, 9,000-tick caps — the full chain fires: 7 skewers / 7 pins / 0 carry-frees / 78 bleeds / 7 frees, **all 7 by CUT**, max pin 525 ticks (≤ one crawl revolution + window — the guarantee holds), accounting exact (skews = pins + carry-frees), 0 anchored-javelin rim deaths, every roll found both fighters' skew state null (the reset LAW), no NaN anywhere; battles ran to cap not OVER (javelin pacing — decided-rate is balance-pass business). Chromecheck **PASS** (row + brief changed). Fingerprints NOT run per #674 — baseline frozen at r42; a sanctioned re-run should expect the javelin cells to move. **Disclosures:** (i) the spin override lands mid-substep, so seam I2's wAngle ledger reads the crawl from the NEXT substep — the one-substep residue is identifiable off the 'skewer'/'skewerFree' event ticks (the pin-escape nudge exemption's pattern); (ii) a bleed-killed victim stays on the wall with the spear through the KO tableau until the roll (deliberate); (iii) during a carry (≤0.45s) the victim outruns the tick-start budget's view — cosmetic tunneling possible, disclosed; (iv) `measure.mjs` counters and the seam section J skewer law are DEFERRED to the bundle per #674. **681 — (2026-11-01) `naildrive-r49` LANDED — THE OWNER'S THIRD SKEWER EYE-RULING, the settled visual sequence: stuck at the hit point, RIGID through the carry, then a forward-only NAIL DRIVE into the rim at arrival.** The owner, after playtesting r48: *"i prefer the javelin firts hit the opponent and the it is visually stuck inside at the point where it hit, then it carries the opponent directly int the rim, and here i want the javelin to go through as the last nail, what i mean it moves into the rim as a nail and only forward from its position, right now it is snapping, it does not look good."* Diagnosis: the impale-r48 during-carry slide moved the spear sideways-through the body while the drag ran at full speed — the concurrent slide was the snap the owner saw. **The settled three-phase sequence:** (1) HIT — the spear sticks at the impact offset, frozen; (2) CARRY — it rides RIGID with the victim (zero relative motion) straight into the rim; (3) DRIVE — at arrival the victim locks at the wall and the spear advances **forward-only along its own flight line** ("only forward from its position") at `SKEWER.driveSpeed` 450 px/s until the head seats just inside the rim at the nail's snap radius (`half − r − 0.5`) — through the body, into the wall, like the crossbow's nails. Fix: the transition now COMPLETES THE RIDE at the dest before arming the drive (the anchor travels the carry's final step with the victim — seamless, and the invariant holds from the transition instant). **682 — (2026-11-01) `sinkalign-r50` LANDED — THE OWNER'S FOURTH SKEWER EYE-RULING, the SINK-AND-ALIGN carry: the grazing class is geometrically dead.** The owner playtested r49 — *"it works as intended"* — but his screenshot (seed 83230, wand×javelin round 1) caught the disclosed grazing-arrival defect live: Speedy pinned at the left wall while the seated spear sat 127px downrange ALONG the wall, 74° off his radial (read-only headless diagnostic at the same seed: 4/4 skewers seated off-radial — 127/116/49/16px along the wall). At the arrival the sink/turn COMPLETE exactly (anchor at the dest, facing = the exact radial — the same seam law as r49's ride-completion), and the forward-only NAIL DRIVE runs from the centre along that radial: ~54px, the head always through the body into the wall, whatever angle the hit came in at. The pinfix-r45 no-flip law is AMENDED, not broken: gradual turn in the carry, freeze at the pin, never an instant flip. The head-on tableau is unchanged (its flight line already WAS the radial; its drive just got shorter); the carry's motion still runs at the fast drag but now INTO the wound, not across it. Disclosed at proposal: grazing carries become impossible by construction. **Edits (asserted anchors, 6 groups):** `startSkewer` stores the arrival radial (`rx0/ry0` — the exit crossing guarantees `ux·rx0 > 0`, so the facing mix never degenerates) and the frozen impact offset (`ox0/oy0`); the carry ride animates `skewOx/Oy → 0` and the facing `û → r̂` per the smoothstep; the arrival block completes both exactly and sets the drive line to the radial; the registry entry amended; the stamps.

**683 — (2026-11-01) `carrydir-r51` LANDED — THE OWNER'S FIFTH SKEWER EYE-RULING: the HEADING CARRY (the drag continues the victim's own travel) and the carry bleed REMOVED.** The owner playtested r50: *"i see it, the motion and all looks nice and smooth i like that, but one thing i didnt like was the sudeen change in direction, id prefer the javelin continue carrying the opponent in the direction it was goin, instead of suddenly turning left or right to hit the rim, also remove the bleed effect while carrying."* Diagnosis: the drag ran along the JAVELIN'S flight line, so a victim travelling across the hit had his path kinked at the contact instant — the sudden direction change. **The settled law (the HEADING CARRY):** the carry line is the VICTIM'S OWN TRAVEL at the contact instant, captured PRE-KNOCK — `shotHitBody`'s knock rides the spear's line, so a post-knock read would bias the drag back toward the javelin's rim point; a near-stationary victim (< 60 px/s, the implementer-picked epsilon) falls back to the spear's own momentum. The rim stops them wherever THAT heading leads; the sinkalign sink/turn/drive machinery is untouched, so the seat stays aligned by construction.
**Two disclosed consequences:** (1) the arrival radial may now sit anywhere up to 180° from the flight line (a victim running away from the throw), so r50's facing turn — a lerp-and-normalize that only worked while the exit crossing guaranteed `û·r̂ > 0` — is upgraded to a SLERP along the shortest signed arc (`k.ta`, precomputed at the hit; never degenerate, same smoothstep easing, freeze at the pin — the pinfix no-flip law still honored); (2) `SKEWER.carryBleed` — the eye-judgment toggle parked for exactly this call — flips to FALSE: bleed pulses only while PINNED (unconditional there per the owner's 100%). **Edits (asserted anchors, 7 groups):** the carryBleed flip; `startSkewer(G, st, foe, pvx, pvy)` + the heading carry line + `hx/hy` + `ta`; the call-site pre-knock capture; the ride's slerp; the skew header comment; the registry (the drag description + the carryBleed ruling); the stamps. **THE SMOKE (the only run, 23s; hammer×javelin s1, bow×javelin s2, dagger×javelin s3, javelin×MIRROR s4):** 50 skewers / 49 pins / 304 bleedTicks / 46 event-frees (45 CUTS) + 4 silent round resets + battle-end residue — the chain exact per battle; **the CARRY-LINE law on every carry tick** (the drag == the victim's heading at the hit, to 1e-12); **ZERO bleedTicks in the carry phase** (asserted per event); 2,274 sink ticks with the offset monotone non-increasing; 2,274 align ticks with the facing dot never regressing and rotation ≤ 50°/tick (the legal slerp worst = 1.5·180°/0.1s/60 ≈ 45°/tick); 716 drive ticks forward-only along the exact arrival radial; **47/47 detected seatings exact** — snap radius to 1e-9 AND on the victim's radial; the buried link bit-exact on every carry tick; victim clamps held; no displacement above 40px/tick; pinned spears frozen; maxPin 472t.
The mirror ran richer (32 skewers — the heading carry follows fleeing victims into walls the flight line would have missed) and hit the 40,000-tick cap in round 3, accounting still exact. **The seed-83230 re-run: the dynamics changed as designed and every seat is still 54.4px victim→head · 0° off-radial · exactly at the snap radius.** Chromecheck skipped (Tier-0 precedent: no label changes). Fingerprints NOT run per #674; the baseline stays frozen at r42. Canonical md5 `819ec63888f8e521117e5bd2525ce9d3` → **`bf6a82640dd09077122f69f6db67c7e8`**, **4,099 lines**, stamps `carrydir-r51`.

**684 — (2026-11-01) `javelincarry-r52` LANDED — THE OWNER'S SIXTH SKEWER EYE-RULING: the carry is the JAVELIN'S direction, and the ZERO-TURN LAW — the r51 misread reversed.** The owner playtested r51: *"i just tested, it looks good except... i think you changed it up, it looks like that atleast, once the javelin hits a target, the targets should get carried by the javelin in the javelins direction, but right now its the target which carries the javelin to the nearest rim in their direction."* **The misread, diagnosed:** r51's *"id prefer the javelin continue carrying the opponent in the direction it was goin, instead of suddenly turning left or right to hit the rim"* was implemented as the VICTIM's heading carry — but *"it"* was the JAVELIN, and the *"sudden change in direction"* was r50's new facing TURN (the swing toward the rim normal), not the victim's path kink at the hit (that kink has existed since r46 and was never the complaint; the turn was r50's only new directional visual). **The settled law:** (1) the carry line is the javelin's flight line again — the victim is dragged where the JAVELIN was going, the rim stops them at the flight line's exit; (2) the ZERO-TURN LAW — the spear's facing never leaves its flight vector through the carry, the arrival and the drive (the whole r50-lerp/r51-slerp turn family is retired; `vx/vy` are simply never touched after the throw — the pinfix-r45 no-flip spirit now trivially total).
**What stays:** the sinkalign SINK (the stuck spear still buries from the impact offset to the body centre on the smoothstep — zero velocity at both ends, the part the owner praised: *"the motion and all looks nice and smooth"*), the forward-only nail drive (now along the flight line from the centre), the exact snap-radius seating, and r51's carry-bleed removal (pin bleed unconditional). **The disclosed tradeoff (owner's eyes judge):** with no turn, a grazing hit seats at the THROW ANGLE again — head through the body centre and into the wall, but offset along the wall instead of perpendicular; extreme grazes (the seed-83230 class) reach ~50–67px along-wall at 45–53°, versus the pre-sink r49 defect's 127px with no through-body at all — the sink keeps the impale readable, the throw angle keeps the motion honest. **Edits (asserted anchors, 7 groups):** `startSkewer` reverted to `(G, st, foe)` with the flight-line carry (the `hx/hy`, `rx0/ry0`, `ta` fields deleted); the call-site pre-knock capture retired; the ride keeps the sink only; the arrival completes the sink at the dest and leaves `s.ux/uy` and the facing untouched; the skew header + the registry amended (both owner quotes recorded); the stamps.

**685 — (2026-11-01) `touchorbit-r53` LANDED — THE BOOMERANG UNPARKED: THE WORLD ORBIT + THE PURE-TOUCH CATCH, from the owner's own solo prototype.** The owner attached a solo prototype preview (his prototype: one gravity-bouncing wielder, arena-centred ellipses solved per throw, *"caught only when it actually touches the wielder's body"*): *"i really like this pattern for bomerang, share your opinion, dont code yet"* — and on my read-back + the one open ruling: *"yes i want you to implement it best possible to fit out project with pure touch, ill look with my eyes again to see if its good, dont look at all the numbers, balancing etc."* **PURE TOUCH was the ruling**: no drift assist, no re-aim, no magnetism — intercepting your own throw is the skill. This SUPERSEDES the parked halo-r39 (its geometric per-circuit catch guarantee was exactly what made the loop feel stapled on — *"the guarantee was the problem"*); the halo's PARK is lifted by landing, not by revert. **The settled law:** (1) THE WORLD ORBIT — every throw solves an arena-centred ellipse through the release point (`orbitThrow`, the prototype's solve + jitter bands ported near-verbatim: a forced past the release radius, b forced inside, four (orientation × sense) solutions, the one whose release tangent best matches the aim wins); the aim is AT THE FOE (implementation choice replacing the prototype's random heading — the throw reads as a throw); the per-throw jitter rides `G.rng` (the sim never touches Math.random) ⇒ an orbit FAMILY, deterministic per seed; the orbit is then FROZEN — world-anchored, owner motion no longer rides along; constant parametric rate om = speed/oa ⇒ the Keplerian read (fastest at the near end: speed·peak = 676 px/s at birth; slowest at the far end ×b/a); the far arc runs OUTSIDE the rim (a ≈ 620 vs 500 — rim-pierce stands, the overflow band earns its keep).
(2) THE PURE-TOUCH CATCH — armed once the throw clears the hand (bodyR + r + CATCH_ARM), retired ONLY inside the bare body window: **CATCH_PAD 20 → 0**; no timer, no expire, no anti-jam machinery BY RULING — an untouched loop simply keeps orbiting. Disclosed at landing (in the row comment): a wielder standing exactly still at the release radius is a permanent ~7px miss (art-centre spawn ≈81px vs the 74px window) — pure touch has no magnetism. **693 — (2026-11-02) `bendspark-r61` LANDED — THE WAND'S BENDING SPARK: the pass's first `trait.turn` axis, on the owner's go (*"ok try and implement it and i will have a look"*).** The wand's spark — the game's fastest shot (800), a pure tracer every defense could pre-solve — now BENDS. Birth turn = `shot.turn` **0.35** rad/s + `trait.turn` **0.085** × min(`f.shotStep`, `TURN_STEPS` **10**) — ceiling **1.2 rad/s ⇒ radius 800/1.2 ≈ 667px**: a swept arena-crossing ARC, never an orbit (orbiting is the boomerang's soul; the cap keeps the bend from clipping it). The SIGN is a fresh per-throw `G.rng()` roll — the pure-chaos doctrine: the wand never aims, it deals a lottery of arcing tracers; growth tilts the lottery toward drama. The draw happens ONLY when the row's bend ≠ 0, so every non-wand rng stream stays byte-identical (proven below). BIRTH-SNAPSHOT law (the effR doctrine): the bend is frozen at spawn, in-flight never re-bends. The flight rides shotStep's constant-rate curve branch — DORMANT since the pre-orbit boomerang retired, WOKEN by this landing: heading rotates `turn·dt` per tick, |v| untouched (the substep budget reads `st.speed` and never sees the bend). Grow trigger = damaging hit under the projectile rule (the wand's spine deals no damage — its hits ARE its sparks). **`bornBend(G,W,f)` is the ONE consumer site**; both spawn lines read it (the old expression's × `dir` spin-sign was the retired pre-orbit boomerang semantics). Boot guards: positive-finite rate, and `shot.turn` ⊥ `shot.returns` — ONE PATH OWNER: the curve branch and the orbit solver must never both claim a row. REGISTRY carries the `trait.turn` entry. Art keeps the SHOT_SPIN tumble — the bend reads in the FLIGHT PATH, not the dart's facing (disclosed; orientation is the owner's eye-call). Rates PROVISIONAL per the process law — no balancing; the owner's eyes judge. Legend: brief '+SPD +BEND / Hit', line 'a spark per rev · the bend deepens with every hit'.

**694 — (2026-11-02) `allcut-r62` LANDED — ALLCUT: EVERY WEAPON CUTS ON BODY TOUCH — the projectile rule RETIRED — plus the owner's eye on the bend: MORE BEND, BIGGER SPARK.** One message, three rulings: *"i think it should bend more and also the wand projectiles should be a bit bigger, and also we need to update so that all weapons from now on deals damage if they touch the opponets body, i believe it is not true for tangential hold, and some more maybe, i cant remember, but it should be true as i realized launchers are boring to watch in the beginning as they have a hard time hitting the opponents and gaining growth"*. **(1) THE LAW:** bladeVsBody's one-line gate `if (W.shot || W.hold === 'tangential') return;` — the projectile rule (owner decision 2026-10-19) plus the shield's tangential no-damage clause — is DELETED.
