Every fresh latched touch is now a damaging hit — HP, knock, hitstop, flash, the hit event and grow() — for launchers and the shield exactly as for melee; the projectile stays the launcher's growth ENGINE but is no longer its only damage. Disclosed, not balanced (#674): launcher spines chip at the WDEF base damage 1; every dormant launcher-side trigger wakes — the shield's `trait.dmg` 0.7 (open question 4: inert since birth, because grow() fires only on a damaging hit and the shield could never land one) fires FOR THE FIRST TIME EVER, the wand's bend deepens on spine touches too, and every shot-growth counter (shotR/cycle/mult/fan/turn) now feeds in the early melee; shield+shield loses its cannot-KO stall and the projectile-mirror termination-red family should shrink — unmeasured (#674 bans); open question 10's 2026-10-21 rationale (*"viewers should be able to understand why bows etc dont do damage in close combat"*) is superseded by the owner himself. Parries and swats untouched; the law-3 resolver and the latch/re-arm machinery untouched. `measure.mjs` law 4's replica and law 7s were comment-aligned to the gate-free law (EDITED, NOT RUN per the owner-go rule; law 7s retired, its counters survive as visibility fields, its PASS line now reads `[----]`). **(2) THE BEND BUMPED at his eye:** base turn 0.35 → **0.9** rad/s (birth radius 889px), growth 0.085 → **0.15** (ceiling 1.2 → **2.4** rad/s ⇒ radius floor **333px** — a hooked arena-crossing arc; the spark dies at the rim long before a 333px circle could close and return, so the orbit doctrine holds: orbiting stays the boomerang's soul). **(3) THE SPARK BIGGER:** `shot.r` 6 → **8** (10.4 drawn/hitbox after weaponScale 1.3). Rates stay PROVISIONAL. **695 — (2026-11-02) `slowmo-r63` LANDED — THE HIT BEAT IS SLOW MOTION: the non-kill hitstop RETIRED, the KO hard freeze KEPT by ruling — and THE WAND IS PARKED at the owner's eye.** The owner watching the post-allcut game: *"we park wand for now, i want to change hitstop to do a brief slowmotion instead, hitstop seems laggy sometimes, when quick multiple hits occur, what do u think"* — the diagnosis agreed with his eye: the freeze gates the sim, so queued contacts re-freeze it the instant it lifts — dense exchanges become a slideshow; allcut-r62's spine chips had just made the hit density far worse. (The file already contained the same diagnosis twice: bleed pulses skip the hitstop because *"freezing the world every 0.43s would stutter the struggle"*, and the r20-era MAX-merge only ever fixed same-tick stops.) A menu was served (window length/rate, refresh flavor, KO exemption) and the owner ruled: *"keep ko hard freeze, and make the slow mo nice and smooth"*. **THE LAW:** a non-kill damaging hit opens a SLOW-MOTION WINDOW — `CFG.hitSlowMs` 150, wall-clock-anchored like the freeze was (4× FF shortens it, the ¼× 's' mode stretches it) — at `CFG.hitSlowRate` **0.25**, deliberately the proven 's'-mode number:
v0.8.2's render interpolation was built for exactly this rate. Windows MAX-merge, never stack: a hit inside slow REFRESHES the window, so a dense exchange reads as one sustained intensity slow — the slideshow class is structurally gone because the sim keeps LIVING inside the beat and queued contacts resolve in slow motion instead of re-freezing it on lift. The KILL beat keeps its HARD FREEZE (`koStopMs` 400 — terminal punctuation with nothing chaining after it; the koStill contact-pose latch and the winner overlay are built around the hold); `slowTicks` clears on kill, on `G.over` and on restart. **THE SMOOTHNESS LAWS** (the owner's *"nice and smooth"*, all three by construction): (1) the expiry frame BLENDS — the fraction of the frame's wall time still inside the window runs at rate, the remainder full-rate: no seam at the transition; (2) sub-tick interpolation stays LIVE — fractional tickAcc at ¼× is the 's'-mode case viewA was built for, so slow motion renders as a pose blend, never steppy; (3) the tick loop does NOT break on a fresh window — the frame finishes its ≤2 integer ticks (sub-frame at 1×) and the slow begins on the next frame's blend, so NO queued tick is ever dropped (the freeze-era break shed up to n−i ticks; only the KO freeze still breaks — its latch semantics want the instant gate).
Wall-time FX (flash decay, banner) live in slow inside the window (`fxRate`), hold at 0 in the KO freeze (pause-r20 kept). **SCOPE:** frame-loop layer ONLY — the sim never reads `slowTicks`/`fxRate`; the sim sequence, the events and every fingerprint are untouched BY CONSTRUCTION (the same guarantee the hitstop comment always made), so no sim smoke ran (#674: lean). `hitStopMs` 60 is DORMANT — one branch in consumeEvents returns it (revert-cheap doctrine). Disclosed: bleed pulses still skip the beat (skewer-r46 unchanged); pause-r20's "true pause" presentation is superseded for non-kill hits only; parry/wall/bounce still never punch (no damage). **THE CHROMECHECK CAUGHT A REAL BUG on the first run:** the `let slowTicks` declaration was missing from the edit batch — the headless loader never runs the frame loop (rAF stubbed), so the boot check passed while the real page threw on the very first frame; the declaration landed, and the lesson is recorded: frame-loop edits are chromecheck-visible or they are untested.

**THE CHROMECHECK (stamp changed ⇒ mandatory; new THREE-CLASS FRAME RECORDER — sim ticks advanced per rAF frame, fight+ko phases — the ko phase matters because the killing blow's tick flips it and the freeze runs after):** PASS1 forced wand+scroll s4321 (the hit-dense cell): battle COMPLETED, zero page errors, stamp `build slowmo-r63`, labels fit — **1,702 full-rate frames (~8 ticks/frame at FF) / 125 SLOW frames (~2 ticks/frame = FF×¼ — the windows engaging on real hits) / 13 FROZEN frames (0 ticks — the KO hard freeze kept)**. PASS2 natural (staff vs katana): COMPLETED, zero errors, 127 slow / 19 frozen. CHROMECHECK PASS.

**THE WAND PARK (the same message): *"we park wand for now"*** — after r62's bump his watch still read *"so boing/underwhelming, its not exciting to watch"*. The bend stays LANDED and provisional; the excitement menu is SERVED, NO PICK yet: **A** the comet pass (renderer-only: a fading trail of the actual flight path so the arc is DRAWN, nose-first orientation instead of the tumble, rim/hit burst flashes) · **B** the lottery bend (magnitude rolled per throw inside a band, not just the sign — every spark a different creature; one rng line, birth-snapshot kept) · **C** flight drama, pick one: DRUNK WOBBLE (heading oscillates on a random birth phase, amplitude grows per hit — the angry firefly) vs TIGHTENING SPIRAL (the turn ramps in-flight — every spark curls tighter as it flies; bends the frozen-in-flight snapshot with the periapsis-speed precedent) · **D** slow the spark 800 → ~600 (longer flights so the drama has room; "fastest shot" was the implementer's pitch, never an owner ruling). Identity ledger checked: none clip ricochet (shuriken), orbit (boomerang), nails (crossbow), skewer (javelin), rain (bow) or fan (knives).

**696 — (2026-11-02) `flagfix-r64` LANDED — THE THREE FLAG LAWS RESTORED: the r61 bendspark comment had SWALLOWED the ricochet/pin/skewer birth spreads at both launch sites — the shuriken's eternal star, the crossbow's nails and the javelin's skewer were dead for exactly three builds. The owner's eye caught it, the slow-mo was EXONERATED, and a second bug (the winner overlay's frozen wall-FX) went with it.** Owner: *"look at javelin, looks like all our pin logic doesnt work anymore since the slow mo update, check other weapons also."*

**THE DIAGNOSIS CHAIN.** The slow-mo took the blame first and was cleared first: the r62-replica twin (every r63 edit reverted) ran 16/16 identity cells BYTE-IDENTICAL against the r63 canonical — the slow-mo had moved nothing, and by construction it couldn't (frame-loop layer only). Then `scratch/pinlaw-check.mjs` ran the r47-era law cells against the r63 canonical AND an r60-replica: shotPin / skewer / ricochet events ZERO in both — the flags were dead BEFORE r60's review too, which cleared the review and pointed at the spawn literals. `scratch/pindiag.mjs` found the smoking gun: crossbow bolts born with `pinFlagTrue: 0` while the runtime row read `WEAPONS.crossbow.shot.pin === true`, and the live-bolt key dump carried NO flag keys at all — the birth spreads never executed.

**THE ROOT CAUSE (self-inflicted, r61).** The bendspark edit's spawn-line `new_text` placed the `// bendspark-r61:` marker BEFORE the three flag spreads — `...(W.shot.ricochet && { ricochet: true })`, `...(W.shot.pin && { pin: true })`, `...(W.shot.skewer && { skewer: true })` — turning all three into comment TEXT at BOTH launch sites (the volley ~2802 and the single ~2819). Dead window: r61→r63. The boomerang survived because its `...(W.shot.returns …)` spread sits on the NEXT line (the r59/r62 catch smokes kept passing — a live false-comfort); wand/bow/knives/staff rows carry no flags. **WHY EVERY GATE MISSED IT:** (1) the r61/r62/r63 smokes contained no shuriken/crossbow/javelin cell; (2) every twin/replica was built FROM the broken canonical, so every diff was blind to the shared base — TWIN DIFFS PROVE DELTA ISOLATION ONLY, NEVER SHARED-BASE LAW LIVENESS; (3) the identity dumps never carried event KINDS (live `G.events` refs, cleared each tick, serialized to `[]`), so no dump could ever show a missing law.

**THE FIX.** Both sites restored — the three spreads as code on their own line (the ricolaw comment kept, a flagfix note added); title/`#build` stamped `flagfix-r64`. A second bug found in the same audit and fixed with it: `fxRate` was assigned only inside the advance block, so after `G.over` it held the KO-freeze `0` forever and the winner overlay's wall-clock FX (flash decay, banner) froze — now a per-frame init `fxRate = stopTicks > 0 ? 0 : 1` before the advance block (chromecheck had missed it: it reads card text, not FX decay). Canonical: md5 `5fbb12d602efbcb8abaa3037332337fe`, 4,355 lines.

**THE PROOF (four instruments).** `scratch/smoke-r64.mjs` — the flag-law battery (5 cells / 340 sim-seconds ≈ 2min wall: a one-time diagnostic-class run, landing smokes stay ~30s): NAILS s4711 shotPin 715 / consumed 68 / swatted 162 / rim deaths **0** / 379,300 rest ticks with **0 moved** (the r45 rest law at full scale); SKEWER s1+s2 9+9 impales / 9+9 rim pins / 84+84 bleed ticks (r47-era scale); ETERNAL STARS s1234 181 bounces / **0 rim deaths / 0 vanish**; BOOMERANG regression s3593 20 catches (the untouched spread, proven untouched). `smoke-r62` re-ran PASS UNCHANGED (bend/allcut/parry laws intact). The identity dump r64-vs-r63 separated SURGICALLY: 6 cells differ / 10 byte-identical, zero anomalies — every differing cell contains a flag weapon (forced crossbow s4711, javelin s777/s83230; natural crossbow×bow, javelin×hammer, shuriken×knives), every identical cell does not — including boomerang×boomerang and boomerang×bow, the diff-level proof the `returns` spread was never hit. Chromecheck 4-pass (stamp changed + frame loop touched ⇒ mandatory): nails live in the real browser (pinned bolts at rest, 87 shotPin events seen, 0 rim deaths), stars bounce (21 seen, 0 rim deaths, 5 still in air at cutoff), skewer fires (victim `f.skew` live across 139 frames, bleeds ticking), the three-class recorder kept (740 full / 41 slow / 4 frozen — the slow-mo and the KO hard freeze survive the fxRate change), the natural match completes with zero page errors — and its own natural roll drew flag rounds mid-match (63 pins, 5 bounces, 0 rim deaths): the laws live unprompted.

**THE STANDING LESSON (law).** Any edit to a code-bearing line must be followed by a law-asserting smoke on EVERY family that line feeds — after a spawn-site edit, shuriken/crossbow/javelin cells are MANDATORY. Twin/replica diffs prove delta isolation only. Identity harnesses that must show event kinds have to shallow-copy events (`G.events.map(e => ({...e}))`) — live refs serialize empty. Law-liveness checks assert events LIVE per tick (the pindiag/pinlaw pattern), never from a dump.

**697 — (2026-11-02) `hexspark-r65` LANDED — THE HEX: the wand's bend became a hunt. The owner's excitement brief drew a five-option menu, his pick was the hunting curse, and his own identity question reframed it — homing as SPITE, not guidance: the victim WEARS the mark, the air turns hostile at once, and the never-orbit law holds by construction.** Owner: *"i need it to be more exciting, like something more exciting to see about its trait, look at the other weapons they have something visually funny/exciting/ridiculous, while it also makes them stronger and people can easily tell ok now they are very op. i dont see the same for wand… there is no real exciting snowball either. i need suggestions and opinion… look at the moment shuriken is one of the strongest and its damage does not increase"*; on the pick: *"i sounds interesting option B, but is that something people conenct to a wand ? … people understand it if the weappn was a heatseeking missile, but i wand do they connect that with homing spell ?"*; the go: *"no just implement it and ill have a look, you can start coding now"*.

**THE BRIEF AND THE MENU.** The diagnosis served first: the game's OP-read runs on four visible languages — COUNT (the rain thickening, the fan widening), SIZE (the grown orbs, the tripled spear), PERSISTENCE/arena furniture (the nails studding the rim, the eternal stars, the loyal halo) and VICTIM DRAMA (the skewer's drag) — and the bend spoke none of them: it changed the shape of one path, subtly, and nothing accumulated. The menu: **A** ANGRY SWARM (the spark splits on hit — the shuriken's count/persistence model, hard caps) · **B** HUNTING CURSE (the bend becomes a seek) · **C** ECHO RITUAL (delayed ghost recasts of the same path) · **D** MOLASSES HEX (victim-side slow — the dark horse, the game's first foe-side growth) · **E** RUNE WALL (wildcard: rim runes that eat enemy shots), plus the comet-trail garnish for any pick. The owner chose **B**; his missile-vs-wand doubt reframed the fantasy — dowsing (the rod that FINDS), D&D's never-miss *magic missile*, the curse archetype (*"hexed"*, *"it follows him"*) make seeking native to the wand when it meanders like a grudge instead of snapping like a guidance kit — so THE HEX LIVES ON THE VICTIM: he wears the counter, the sparks hunt the hexed man. The word "homing" appears nowhere near the weapon.

**THE LAW (PROVISIONAL rates, the owner's eye pending).** The turn budget splits per substep: the birth bend keeps authority (1−h), a SEEK toward the hexed foe gets h = SEEK_H0 0.3 + (1−SEEK_H0)·min(own.shotStep, TURN_STEPS)/TURN_STEPS (spellform-r69: the seek floor — spells hunt from BIRTH at the owner's hit-rate ruling; the h=0 byte-identity claim is retired by that ruling) — LIVE, not frozen at birth: when the hex deepens, ALL sparks in the air turn hostile at once (the snowball the brief asked to SEE). AUTHORITY IS CONSERVED: |(1−h)·turn + h·|turn|·s| ≤ |turn| ≤ 2.4, so the never-orbit law (333px radius floor — orbiting is the boomerang's soul) holds BY CONSTRUCTION, `st.turn` is never rewritten (the r61 frozen-birth law stands), and at h=0 the applied turn is `st.turn` EXACTLY — the early game is the r62 bend byte-for-byte. NO new rng draws: the seek reads positions, so every rng stream (the wand's included) stays untouched. `SEEK_BAND` 0.7 rad PROVISIONAL — the seek ramps proportionally inside the band and saturates outside: a committed spark flies straight, no chatter across the aim line, and the wide lazy arcs keep it jukeable (a curse you can outrun, not a lock). THE MARK: the victim wears a pulsing ring in the ATTACKER's colour ("his magic is on you"), thickening and standing off with the stacks, an inner bleed past half — the pulse rides `G.phaseT`, so the curse's heartbeat throbs inside the slow-mo window and freezes with the KO hold. THE COMET TRAIL (the menu's garnish, folded in): hex sparks draw their recent path as a fading tail in the owner's colour — interpolated drawn positions, dead flights fade over a few frames, `TRAIL_N` 0 disables (cheap revert); half of *"so boing/underwhelming"* was a fast tracer with no path language. Boot guard: `shot.hex` requires `shot.turn` (the hex rides the bend axis). Brief `+SPD +HEX / Hit`; line 'a spark per rev · every hit deepens the HEX — the sparks begin to hunt the hexed'. Damage UNCHANGED; no balancing per the standing ruling; renderer layers are read-only on the sim.

**THE PROOF.** `scratch/smoke-r65.mjs` PASS 3.8s: 7,428 spark ticks all carrying the hex flag; 0 frozen-turn violations; max |turn| exactly the 2.4 cap; 23 HUNT WITNESSES of 41 observed at full hex (the heading error to the foe shrinks over a 0.2s window); 32 hits, 0 vanish, 0 NaN, maxStep 22; the wand×javelin cell proves the hex coexists with the skewer law (3 skewers). `smoke-r62` ALIGNED (edit-only, the measure.mjs precedent): its curve checker asserted the r62 constant-rate law literally, which the hex legitimately supersedes — now h=0 keeps the EXACT check and h>0 asserts the AUTHORITY BOUND |dh| ≤ |st.turn|/hz (the hunt direction is smoke-r65's witness job): PASS, curve 5,776 checks / 0 viol, every other r62 law untouched (parries 37, catches 11, spark ends all >0). `smoke-r64` RE-RAN per the r64 STANDING LAW (the spawn line was touched again): PASS with numbers BYTE-IDENTICAL to the r64 landing — 715 pins / 9+9 skewers / 181 bounces / 20 catches: the hex flag rides the restored line without brushing the flag families. Chromecheck 3-pass (labels changed + two new renderer paths — headless stubs rAF, so these prove themselves in chrome or not at all): P1 forced wand×scroll s4321 COMPLETES with zero page errors, the hex on 1,418/1,418 spark frames, turns frozen, labels exact, fit clean, the three-class recorder kept (1,939 full / 134 slow / 12 frozen — slow-mo + KO freeze survive); P2 the nails browser-side regression (40 shotPin, 0 rim deaths); P3 natural completes, zero errors, stamps — its rolls exercised the trail hygiene (resetFx/reveal clears). Harness disclosures: the first P2 gate failed on a missing counter increment IN THE PROBE (game-side numbers were already green — fixed, re-ran), and the volatile /tmp puppeteer environment was rebuilt mid-check (npm + apt shared libs).

Canonical: md5 `5a75da5379c481455e60da7f99bbde52`, 4,450 lines, stamps `hexspark-r65`. The unpicked menu options (swarm / echo / molasses / rune wall) stay available as future axes — the hex spent none of their identities.

**698 — (2026-11-02) `affinity-r66` LANDED — THE TOME TRIO IS LIVE: the 2026-10-28 ruling ("the three kinds become mechanically distinct in the skills pass") executed — halo POISONS (a stacking DoT), nova CHILLS (a floored tempo slow), vortex SWELLS (the capped birth size) — landed together with the owner-designed SLOT LAW, the stacking rulings and the STATUS-RING GRAMMAR that makes debuffs/DoTs readable as one family.** Owner: *"ok we work on tome then"* → *"ok try and implement the tome for me to see it in action"*; the design session: *"do you think there should be a limit to one dot on a bot at any time, if it is hit by another dot… it will then replace the prior dot ? so always the most reason one, and also waht about stacking, should that be allowed ? like stacking 5 poisions etc"* and the visual law: *"we should keep in mind is to have the same visuals for debuff/dots… like lets just for exmaple take the hexx ring, the only thing that changes is the color, not the shape"*; and the flag: *"we will also implement healing later, which just off the top of my head should be a 'buff'"*. The hex itself was eye-blessed at the same word (*"i watched it and i like it a lot"*).

**THE DESIGN-SESSION RULINGS SERVED (all accepted).** (1) THE SLOT LAW: ONE DoT slot + ONE debuff slot per bot — the same status refreshes and DEEPENS (never a second instance), a different DoT taking the slot REPLACES with stacks reset (a fresh curse counts from one), and slots NEVER evict each other — the full "most recent wins" model was argued down because the strict cycle would self-cancel (every frost page would wipe the poison the previous page applied; the book must compound, not fight itself). 1v1 means ONE attacker, so cross-weapon status wars are structurally impossible: no immunity, no purge, no priority tables, no generic status engine — two shallow victim fields (`f.dot`, `f.chill`) are the whole system. (2) STACKING: depth YES (cap 5 — the owner's own number, house uniform-cap pattern), multiple instances NO; the tick RHYTHM stays fixed and the stacks scale the BITE and the ring's thickness (a learnable beat; a speeding pulse reads jittery). Duration-based with clean fall-off, round-reset LAW applies. (3) THE RING GRAMMAR (the hex mark is the house template): ONE design, fixed inside-out slots — DoT tight against the body (it is eating him), the curse band outside, the BUFF slot outermost (reserved for the healing); colour = WHICH status (poison green KP1 / frost blue KN1 — the shipped affinity tints; healing must later avoid the cast golds AND the poison green — ledger discipline); thickness + stand-off = DEPTH; pulse = TEMPO, debuffs beat HARD and INWARD, buffs will breathe soft and outward (valence rides the motion, not just the colour — colourblind redundancy); at the cap the ring gains the inner bleed — the MAXED flourish (the hex precedent). RINGS ARE ONLY FOR CARRIED STATES — an instant effect is a flash, not a ring (the boundary that keeps the system from becoming an FX dump). The owner will design the GENERIC ring himself and show it (*"ills figure out myself and design a generic debuff 'ring' or similar to what you did with the hex, and ill show you"*) — the trio's rings are the placeholder that became the contract: his redesign swaps the paint, not the plumbing.

**THE LAW (all rates PROVISIONAL, the owner's eye pending).** AFFINITY binds cycle kind → element (halo poison · nova frost · vortex fire — kind-keyed, and the trio kinds are tome-only art keys, so no other shot can trip a payload; boot-guarded: every cycle kind must have an element). FIRE: the vortex page's birth radius grew LINEAR off the shared `f.shotStep` at trait.shotR 0.18, capped at TRIO.steps 5 — **spellform-r69 AMENDMENT at the owner's eye (*"should be same shape except their color... vortex is bigger than the others"*): the SIZE AXIS IS DORMANT (`TRIO.fireSwell` 0; the rate retired into `TRIO.fireGrow` 0.18 behind the switch, trait.shotR dropped from the tome row, boot guard cycle⊥shotR) — ONE PAGE DESIGN IN THREE INKS is now the ART LAW, and fire keeps colour only until the owner's generic status design gives it a lane (a fire DoT would collide with poison's DoT slot by the replace law — his call).** The staff keeps its uncapped compounding (shotR's ONE consumer since r69). The row's new shotR also switched the tome onto the shared counter (grow()'s axis gate needed no edit). POISON: a biting DoT — every 40 ticks (~0.33s) for 0.35×stacks hp, 8s lifetime; dotStep mirrors skewBleed exactly (per-TICK rhythm, hp clamp, death + deathSeq, honest events). FROST: a tempo slow — chillMul() = max(FLOOR 0.6, 1 − 0.07×stacks) read at THE WEAPON SWING (chillfix-r71, owner ruling: *"freeze/slow effect should only apply to the weapon… i dont want the bot to fall to the floor"* — the movement site is REMOVED, the body never drags; the per-rev cast cadence follows the slowed swing as its only honest consequence), 6s lifetime; THE FLOOR LAW: never a full lock (a dragging man is funny, a statue is not a fight). **THE SUSTENANCE LAW (found by the smoke, disclosed):** the first run FAILED its own deepen-assert — the strict cycle self-diluted (every 3rd page is poison, and blades swat pages, so a per-element refresh starved the stacks — venom lapsed between poison landings). Fix: ANY page hit refreshes BOTH statuses' lifetimes (the book's pressure sustains its curses), while only the element's OWN page deepens — symmetric with fire riding the shared counter; ttls lengthened to 8s/6s to match the real cadence (which quickens as +SPD grows — refreshes turn plentiful as the match heats: the snowball arc). Events: poison/poisonTick/dotEnd/chill/chillEnd (measure.mjs alignment joins the deferred debt — edit-only policy). NO new rng draws. chillMul returns EXACTLY 1 with no chill (x*1===x), and non-cycle rows keep the shotgrow effR bit-identical (pR === effR) — every non-tome match is provably untouched. Renderer: the two status rings in drawBody (anticipation wind-up off d.acc, the bite snap off poisonPulse — fed by poisonTick in consumeEvents, decayed on the fxRate'd wall clock: the rings throb in slow-mo and freeze at the KO), reset with the roll. Brief '+SPD +TRIO / Hit'; line 'pages cycle POISON · FROST · FIRE — venom stacks and ticks, frost chills to the floor, fire pages swell'. Boot guards: TRIO positive-finite, chillFloor in (0,1], AFFINITY coverage.

**THE PROOF.** `scratch/smoke-r66.mjs` PASS 4.1s (tome×tome s21 mirror + tome×bow s22, 90s each): venom deepened to n=2, 62 bites all at the exact 0.35×n formula (0 viol), chill deepened to n=2, dotEnd/chillEnd lifecycles fired, **coexistTicks 623 — the SLOT LAW witnessed (one victim wearing DoT AND debuff)**, the fire ladder complete (7.8 → 9.204 → … → 14.82 = exactly the ×1.9 cap), the element gate 0 violations (halo/nova NEVER swell), roll-wipe clean, NaN 0. Regressions BYTE-IDENTICAL: smoke-r65 (hex witnesses 23/3, skewer coexistence 3), smoke-r62 PASS, smoke-r64 PASS with the exact r64 numbers (715 pins / 9+9 skewers / 181 bounces / 20 catches) — the spawn-literal touch (r: effR → r: pR) re-ran the flag battery per the r64 STANDING LAW and moved nothing. Chromecheck 2-pass PASS (labels changed + two new renderer paths): P1 the forced mirror COMPLETED in-window with zero page errors through rings/rolls/KO, the three-class recorder kept (1,511 full / 99 slow / 13 frozen), labels exact, fit clean, stacks/coexistence/fire-cap all witnessed in the live DOM (92 coexist frames, maxVortexR 14.82); P2 natural completed AND rolled its own tome round unbidden — chill deepened to 2, 4 venom bites, 101 coexistence frames, vortex capped: the trio lives in natural play.

**701 — (2026-11-02) `hexfam-r68` LANDED — THE FAMILY FLIGHT LAW: THE HEX IS NO LONGER THE WAND'S TRAIT — IT IS WHAT MAGIC DOES.** Owner: *"from now on all magic/spell users should adopt the same mechanig as wand currently have, it looks good, what do you think, but then we just need to find something unique for the wand"* → the go: *"yes go ahead and do the revert and implement the new 'flight' law for all magic users, also staff. once done we will discuss what trait wand will now get as it is left with no trait, since its 'flight' was its trait before"*. The doctrine this completes (the #699 ledger, answered as a CLASS law instead of three bespoke patches): **STEEL OWNS PRESENCE, MAGIC OWNS INEVITABILITY.**

**THE LAW.** Every `shot.hex` row — wand, tome, scroll, staff (the magic users; the ORB WEAPON is a sentinel, every steel launcher flies straight) — is born bent and hunts the hexed. bornBend now DERIVES the ladder off the row's own speed: `(HEXFAM.base 0.9 + HEXFAM.grow 0.15 × min(f.shotStep, TURN_STEPS 10)) × speed ÷ HEXFAM.ref 800` — the wand's blessed r62/r65 numbers ARE the family standard, and the radius language is speed-independent: every spell births on the same ~889px arc and tightens to the same ~333px hunt floor at the cap, so the never-orbit identity wall (the boomerang's soul) holds family-wide BY CONSTRUCTION. The sign is a fresh per-throw `G.rng` roll (pure chaos — magic never aims); non-hex rows take the falsy branch with ZERO rng draws (streams byte-identical). **THE WAND IS BIT-IDENTICAL**: k = 800/800 = 1 exactly, x*1 === x — the flight he eye-blessed (*"i watched it and i like it a lot"*) is untouched; it just stopped being HIS. The r65 machinery needed no edits — the seek's authority split (h — floored at SEEK_H0 0.3 since spellform-r69, SEEK_BAND 0.7), the victim's mark in the ATTACKER's colour and the comet trails were row-generic as written; the tome KEEPS its TRIO (flight × payload are orthogonal — pages poison/chill/swell AND hunt). The scroll's trait slot stays open (UNROLLING served; the flight law is not a trait — the same ruling as the wand's).

**THE GROW() GATE DISCOVERY (disclosed — the first battery run FAILED on r65 and convicted a consumer nobody had listed).** The wand's flight was bit-exact but its COUNTER was dead: grow() advances the shared `f.shotStep` only for rows declaring a shot-growth TRAIT axis (`ab.shotR || ab.cycle || ab.mult || ab.fan || ab.turn`), so retiring `trait.turn` dropped the wand off its own counter (maxStep 0 → no deepening, no hunt, r65's witnesses 23→0) — and the scroll was never on it at all. The fix made the gate read the family law itself: `ab.turn` → `WEAPONS[f.weapon].shot.hex` (the hex deepens off the counter — the counter serves it). The wand's counter timeline is identical (the gate opens on exactly the same hits); the scroll joins for the first time. The lesson joins the house ledger beside flagfix-r64: **when retiring a field, hunt its CONSUMERS, not just its declarers.**

**RETIREMENTS + GUARDS.** `trait.turn` and `shot.turn` are RETIRED — no reads remain, and boot guards THROW if either is ever declared again (dead fields rot silently — the r64 lesson); `shot.hex` requires a PLAIN flight path (⊥ returns/ricochet/pin/skewer — one path owner per row); the r61 turn-validator, the r61 turn⊥returns and the r65 hex⇒turn guards are superseded. The REGISTRY's trait.turn entry is rewritten as the retirement record; two stale comments corrected in passing (the contact-rules header had claimed "the boomerang alone curves" since before r61; the hex-mark comment now speaks for the family). **THE WAND'S TRAIT SLOT IS OPEN** — its crown pends the owner's discussion (his words above). "Bend more" was rejected as the lever and is disclosed here: the birth bend's sign is pure chaos (magic doesn't aim, it arrives) — bending harder scatters, it doesn't hit; the seek channel is the aim, so the hit-rate lever is the floor. Authority stays conserved (|tv| ≤ |turn| ⇒ the never-orbit wall holds by construction); the h=0 byte-identity with the r62 bend is RETIRED BY HIS RULING (he owns the flight — smoke-r62 re-aligned to the surviving authority bound, its exact-match branch retired with the claim it guarded).

**716 — (2026-10-31) `kindlingfix-r83` LANDED — THE EMBER SPILL TUNED AT HIS EYE: THE BIG SPOT KEEPS BITING, THE SPILL THINS OUT AND SCATTERS, THE FADING BITE BECOMES VISIBLE.** His verdict blessed the mechanic — *"this is mechanic is good i think, we just need to tweak it"* — with four tweaks in one breath. (1) *"when a meteor hits its target it leaves behind a bigger burning spot, thats good, but i want the spot to damage multiple times when enemies are standing on it, just like when someone is pinned by javelin against the wall, they take continuous damage, and not just once, but only the bigger spot"*: the pool ALWAYS ticked (every 0.2s while stood in) — the dead read was the hit's own knock (hitKnock 130) sliding the victim off the fire after ~1 beat (the smokes' 1.3 burns/pool); pinning stays the javelin's soul (family law: no identity theft), so the DIALS moved instead — POOL.tick 24 → 16 (a beat every ~0.13s), POOL.reach 0.5 → 0.75 (the zone reads to 62 + 48 = 110px — the knocked slide stays in the fire) — and the coals KEEP their one-shot pop ("only the bigger spot" bites repeatedly). (2) *"the small spots which spills out fro the meteor/projectile, should not spawn that frequently, but still not too slow"*: the cadence halves — drop 14 → 30, dropCut 1 → 2, dropMin 6 → 14 (a coal every 0.25s of flight at stack 0 → every ~0.12s at the cap; the snowball survives). (3) *"they should spawn at random rappterns around the meteor not just behind it like a tail"*: EMBER.scatter 44 — each coal lands on a RANDOM DISC around the flight point (two row-owned G.rng draws at the drop — angle + sqrt-radius for a uniform disc, the bornBend precedent; rim-clamped: no coal burns off the floor). (4) *"the opponent should take damage stepping on these, even though they are fading away, im not sure i think i saw no damage as it was fading"* — DISCLOSED: the bite never faded (the sim pops to t=0); the READ did — the pop had no art. **721 — (2026-11-10, RECORDED RETROACTIVELY) `links-r87` LANDED — THE ADDING-LINKS CHAIN: THE FLAIL GROWS BY ADDING LINKS, NEVER BY STRETCHING ART; THE r86 PITCH STRETCH + `growMax` ARE SUPERSEDED WHOLE.** The owner invented and landed this build between sessions, undocumented — his word at the recording: *"i have invented the link stretch, just not documented it"* (2026-11-10 chat); this entry is written from the canonical's own comment blocks and code (the `ext` comment above `FLAIL_CH` is the authoritative in-file record), and no creation-time quotes exist beyond #720's pick and this sentence. **THE LAW.** `FLAIL_CH.ext = { pitch: 1, unit: 28, every: 4, max: 3, ring: 3, bar: 4 }` — one growth UNIT is one ring+bar pair (shapes `ring` 3 and `bar` 4, copied `unit` 28u further out in S2's frame with the same ring/bar thread overlap as the base links), so S2 (J1..J2) lengthens by 28u per unit and every later joint rides outward with it; the head segment S3 is untouched (pitch 24u, art unscaled — nothing stretches; the haft and eye never scale). One unit per `every` 4 damaging hits on the shared `f.lenStep`, at most `max` 3 units.
**THE LADDER, RECONCILED.** The flail consumes the SAME uniform `LEN_STEPS` 12 cap as the spear's ladder — 12 / every 4 = max 3 units — but takes no `LEN_LADDER` row and no re-bake: `chainGrow` now returns the INTEGER count of added units (0 = the shipped chain) and remains the ONE growth read by sim (flailPose/flailStep), hitbox (bladeCircles) and art (drawWeaponChain), so the three can never disagree. **STRUCTURE.** A grown flail has np = 3 + 2n particles (`chainNp`: S1 ring, S2 bar, then n × (ring, bar), then the head) — every added link is its own hinged segment with its own frame; `chainSegBase` maps each frame to the authored segment whose art + covering it wears (ring → base S1, bar → base S2), and `ch.circBySeg` holds the baked coverings per BASE segment — every added link re-uses its TYPE's baked list in its own frame, so the art↔hitbox tie stays frozen per type and the inscribe gate holds at every step by construction (the #720 guarantee, kept). Pitches are all the base link pitch (14u) except the head's (24u). Per-particle inverse masses are recomputed for the grown chain (`ch.iwTab`: light links 1/0.3, heavy head 1/1.4).
**REACH.** `ch.ext.px = unit × CFG.weaponScale` (one added pair, in px) and the chain REACH pass adds `ext.max × ext.px` — the grown chain reaches 3 units further. **BOOT GATE (new).** `ext.max` must cover `floor(LEN_STEPS / ext.every)` or the chain would silently stop growing before the step cap — loud throw at boot, never a silent truncation. This replaces #720's `growMax` 1.39 art-connectivity gate, DELETED with the stretch mechanism it bounded. **AUDIT.** The chain-pose audit widened: `auditFl` stride 13 → 37 (sub + 18 + 18 — the per-fighter chain pose grew 6 → 18 floats since np reaches 9); `auditSubs` (stride 13) unchanged. **ROW.** trait.len 0.025 unchanged · brief '+DMG +LEN / Hit' unchanged · line updated to 'a loose chained ball · gains damage per hit · a new chain link every 4 hits — the ball flies further out'. **CANONICAL IDENTITY.** md5 `afc30118054c81265ade159224c9f03f`, 5,392 lines (r86 was `ae528b2e12197dc4eacbd383215d7421`, 5,176). **KNOWN COMMENT DEBT (in the canonical, untouched — a comment-only edit would move the md5 this entry pins, so it awaits an owner-sanctioned micro-landing):** the flail row's trailing comment still names the r86 pitch stretch + `growMax` as current, the REGISTRY's trait.len stanza still reads "CHAIN weapons (the flail, longchain-r86) scale ONLY the pitches … boot-gated by ch.growMax", and the `mul` history comment above `chainNp` describes the r86 factor without a superseded marker — the authoritative r87 description is the `ext` comment block, which does carry its own "historical note, superseded" marker.
**PROOF — NONE ON RECORD; verification owed.** The build landed without a smoke or fingerprint run on record. A links-law smoke is owed at the next flail touch.

THE WAND CAME OFF PARK at the owner's excitement brief (*"i need it to be more exciting… people can easily tell ok now they are very op… there is no real exciting snowball either"*): the five-option menu (angry swarm / hunting curse / echo ritual / molasses hex / rune wall + the comet garnish) was served, he PICKED the hunting curse, and his identity question (*"people understand it if the weappn was a heatseeking missile, but i wand do they connect that with homing spell ?"*) reframed it as THE HEX — spite, not guidance; the r63-era park menu is superseded, its comet garnish folded into the landing (#697). ALLCUT — EVERY WEAPON CUTS ON BODY TOUCH (owner ruling 2026-11-02: *"all weapons from now on deals damage if they touch the opponets body… launchers are boring to watch in the beginning as they have a hard time hitting the opponents and gaining growth"*): the projectile rule (2026-10-19) and the shield's 'parries only' clause are RETIRED — the gate is gone from bladeVsBody; launcher spines chip at base damage 1 and feed EVERY shared growth counter (the shield's trait.dmg 0.7 fired for the first time ever; the wand's bend deepens on spine touches too); parries/swats/latch/resolver untouched; measure.mjs aligned (edited, not run; law 7s retired). The wand's spark bends at the r62 rates — birth turn **0.9** + **0.15** per damaging hit on the shared `f.shotStep`, capped at `TURN_STEPS` 10 (ceiling **2.4** rad/s ⇒ radius floor **333px** — a hooked arc, never a closed orbit); spark r **8** (10.4 scaled); the per-throw `G.rng` sign drawn ONLY for bent rows (non-wand streams byte-identical — PROVEN at r61: 14/16 replica-twin cells identical); `st.turn` frozen at birth — AND spends that same budget under THE HEX (r65): the bend keeps (1−h), the seek toward the hexed foe gets h (LIVE — all in-air sparks turn hostile at once), `SEEK_BAND` 0.7 rad PROVISIONAL, authority conserved so the never-orbit law holds by construction and the old h=0 identity with the r62 bend is retired at spellform-r69 (SEEK_H0 floors h); no new rng draws; |v| untouched; the victim wears the pulsing attacker-coloured mark and the spark trails its comet (renderer-only); boot guard hex⇒turn. grudge-r70's THE LONG GRUDGE was REVERTED WHOLE at chillfix-r71 (read as tome-like linger), flick-r72's WHIP-CRACK FLICK was REVERTED WHOLE at bloom-r73 (the second projectile-tuning veto) — and bloom-r73 crowned the wand with TRANSFIGURATION: the struck weapon becomes a joke object that does nothing at all (#706). Rates PROVISIONAL; his eyes judge. THE BOOMERANG IS DONE (owner: "ok boomerang is done for now"; r58 eye: "so it looks good now"). Its settled law: the catch is the loop's only OWNER-side end and rethrows INSTANTLY (RETHROW_WAIT 0, the r54 beat dormant in the constant); blades pass; enemy projectiles DIE on the drawn arc; LOOP MEETS LOOP BOTH DIE; same-bot pairs pass through (owner-blind law, ready for a future multi-loop balance); the hitbox IS the drawn arc including its tumble; held art ALWAYS visible; the catch ghost rides home; arc-aware arm; cycle growth on the catch; PURE-CHAOS throws on the frozen world orbit (b 70); one loop per bot in the air. The javelin's settled law: STUCK at the hit → the JAVELIN'S-DIRECTION carry with the sink and ZERO-TURN facing → forward-only NAIL DRIVE; no bleed carrying.** **Traits 14 of 22 — 8 remain.** Trait slots left:
the 7 remaining MELEE BLANKS — hammer, axe, sword, mace, nunchaku, scythe, katana (POISON is ledger-claimed by the tome's halo page; RED is reserved for the bleed retrofit) + the ORB, then the bot cast, then balance — his order. The owner's own GENERIC STATUS-RING design is promised ("ill show you") — the trio's rings are the placeholder that became the contract. The knock seam waits on the owner's which-knock decision. PARKED: the scroll's dial C (floor density — cap vs cadence) stays pocketed at his closure (#719 — "the mechanics are in", the OP read acknowledged and parked for the balance pass); the E-menu leftovers (flare capstone, meteor wake) stay parked — no landing without a fresh ruling. BOTH MAGIC JOURNEYS ARE CONCLUDED: the wand (#710) and the scroll (#719 — THE EMBER SPILL BLESSED, one mechanic through four builds, two revert scars). NEXT: the owner's eye on the flail's adding-links chain (rate 0.025, a link every 4 hits, max 3 units — all PROVISIONAL, #721); then the remaining 7 melee blanks + the orb at his pick (the hammer was the served recommendation; the flail landed first at his word), bot cast, balance — his order.

## Testing recipe (headless)

**Owner ruling 2026-10-28 — the proof battery is TIERED, not per-landing.** Owner, with more skills and bots queued: *"i really dont think all this testing is nescessary, we are adding a lot more skills and bots, so if you do this each time its going to take forever."* He is right, and the cause was habit: most of the battery exists because the length-growth builds re-baked geometry, which a shot-multiplier or knock skill does not. So, in force from `skill-r29` onwards — **Tier 0, every landing (~1 min):** a boot probe (does it load, do the numbers read right, does the hook actually fire) plus `.qa/lengrow-build-proof.mjs`, plus `chromecheck` only if a label or legend changed. **Tier 1, only if geometry moved** (art, polys, coverings, scale): `inscribe.mjs`, `lengrow-bake --verify`, `leadproof`, `flipproof`. **Tier 2, only on suspicion or once at the end of the pass:** differential sweeps, cost measurements, fingerprints. Measured-facts entries get correspondingly **shorter** — what changed, the numbers that matter, what is owed — not fifteen sub-points. What is *not* cut: a measurement that can **change a decision**. The carrier choice for the first special skill was settled by five minutes of measurement that falsified the obvious pick (Heavy is self-defeating; see the `skill-r29` entry), which is design work rather than verification, and it stays.

The harness conventions carried over from the abandoned project — they caught every real bug there, and two here on day one (the stall tail, the missing buzzsaw). All harnesses drive `spinclash-preview.html` (canonical filename — harnesses default to it).

**STANDING RULE (owner, 2026-10-19): `measure.mjs` NEVER runs without the owner's explicit go — "dont run measure.mjs. (atleast not untill i say so, ever. ask if you want to)."** Ask first; use the fast probe for iteration. **The rule was exercised 2026-10-28:** the owner asked for the run, then ran Parts 0-4 himself on his own Windows machine while the sandbox took Parts 5-6. **Split-baseline procedure, now proven end to end:** `--cells a+b,c+d --matrix-only` skips naturals and determinism, and the parts' battle sets are disjoint so the law counters sum exactly; cut the 484 cells from the roster the harness itself prints (`--vs nosuchweapon` exits 2 and lists all 22), keep each command well inside cmd.exe's 8,191-character limit (81 cells is about 1.2 KB), give every part its own output file, and aggregate with `scratch/measure-agg.mjs` (read-only — sums counters, maxes extremes, re-derives winner splits, and asserts the parts share no cell). **Redirect with `>`: the harness writes no files of its own**, there is not one write call in it — re-verified 2026-10-28 after the attribution patch: **0 write calls at 1,216 lines**. On Windows use cmd.exe — Git Bash's `tee` died with the VS Code terminal (`stdout is not a tty`) and PowerShell converts the harness's stderr progress ticker into `NativeCommandError` text that looks like a crash. Cost: 26-73 min per 81-cell part at 8 workers, 124 min for a 79-cell part at 2; two workers measured no faster in aggregate than one on a 2-core box, so `--jobs` buys nothing there. Harness md5 `3ba90d73a502bcef6313111546740d30` since the law-3 field fix. Since v1.2 the harness is parallel (worker threads — the report is proven byte-identical at any `--jobs` count) and tiered; a full matrix run is ≈3,900 battles at the 22-weapon roster (484 cells × 8), wall ≈2.5h ÷ cores. **Sandbox reality (learned 2026-10-19, the hard way):** the assistant's sandbox cannot host matrix-scale runs — background processes are silently reaped, foreground calls cap at 30 min, and killed runs masquerade as stalls. Matrix-scale baselines belong on the owner's machine as one command (minutes there), or in owner-sanctioned `--matrix-only` chunks.

**Layout — every command below runs bare from the repo root.** All harness defaults resolve `previews/spinclash-preview.html`, and `navgen`/`docreflow` default to `docs/GAMES.md`. Explicit-arg semantics are deliberately unchanged and differ by folder: the `.qa`/`scratch` harnesses resolve an arg from the repo root (`inscribe` must, because `bake-rows` passes it `scratch/.bake-temp.html`), while `measure.mjs` and `chromecheck.mjs` live at the repo root and resolve an arg relative to their own folder (the folder-relative rule stands for any explicit arg).

```
node measure.mjs [file] [seeds=40] [--verbose]
                 [--quick | --full | --vs <weapon> | --cells a+b,c+d | --solidity] [--matrix-only] [--jobs N]   # OWNER'S GO ONLY
node chromecheck.mjs [file]                                     # ~4s, safe
node .qa/inscribe.mjs [file]                                            # ~6s, safe — PERMANENT inscription gate (owner directive
                                                                        # 2026-10-19: events only on visible contact). Partial art
                                                                        # edits re-bake via `.qa/bake-rows.mjs <file> <ids>` (row-splice,
                                                                        # keepers byte-identical) then this gate; --regen is WHOLE-fleet
                                                                        # re-derivation only, never a splice source (orb-r14 incident)
node ingest.mjs <file.svg> [--len N] [--id name] [--name Name] [--into previews/spinclash-preview.html] [--seeds N]   # DORMANT since roster-r12 — the kit route is the live path
```

Situational probes retained in `.qa/`: `fingerprint.mjs` (refactor-neutrality proof — run before/after, diff the JSONs), `lengrow-proof.mjs` (**added 2026-10-28** — the length-growth ladder's coverage probe: read-only, ~3s, writes nothing and runs no battles. Loads the canonical through the `leadproof.mjs` loader, rebuilds a weapon's runtime poly, and sweeps the `d`-only stretch factor `k`, reporting `eps(k)` (the house sliver metric, per `ingest.mjs` :462-469) beside `touch(k)` (the deepest a body disc can overlap the drawn silhouette while missing every covering circle — the 2026-10-19 "gap hit") and the derived tip model `protrusion(k) = (tipD − lastD)·k − lastR`. This is the instrument that proved the twelve ladder steps must be **baked** rather than stretched at runtime. Positionals: canonical path, then weapon id (default `spear`); `--k` overrides the sweep list, `--steps`/`--rate` the ladder), `leadproof.mjs` (the edge-leading mirror's birth proof + standing regression, owner decision 2026-10-22 — Part A: the declared business contour leads the motion at every angle × both spin signs — on the weapon's mirror axis: chisel EDGE and hook BEAK laterally (`lead`), spear HEAD-END along the chord (`headLead`, the javelin); Part A2: the drawn art satisfies the hitbox's exact mirrored mapping; Part B: flagged cells' hits/parries/swats recompute from stored event fields alone. **Extend its PROBES/CELLS for every new `lead` weapon**), `flipproof.mjs` (the edge-leading flip TRANSITION's proof, owner decision 2026-10-22 — T1 shape: episodes leave ±1 only for flagged fighters, pass through near edge-on, land exactly ±1; T2 the unflagged side and an all-unflagged control never animate, with sim-scan non-vacuity both directions; T3 the bell snap: nothing animates before the first flagged parry; T4 the window is bounded by `CFG.flipMs`. DOM-stub frame loop reading `info().flip`; the glint it replaced is retired with its record in Measured facts). **RETIRED 2026-10-28 (pre-skills workspace cleanup, owner go): `natscan.mjs`, `boomdiag.mjs`, `reveal-probe.mjs`, `killstill.mjs` and `.qa/chainlaw.mjs` were spent one-shot diagnostics and are deleted — every finding stays in Measured facts. `killstill` proved the K.O. kill still that was itself removed at owner word 2026-10-21 ("i dont want it to be announced"), and `reveal-probe` proved a reveal that has since shipped and is guarded by `chromecheck.mjs`. `chainlaw.mjs` attributed the 2026-10-27 red set to harness chain-blindness and its coverage now lives inside `measure.mjs` as the `missing chain poses` law (0 across the 484-cell baseline), so the standalone probe is redundant; if a future law red needs re-attributing to chain blindness, that is the instrument to rebuild.** Its full spec, preserved verbatim here because the file is gone and a rebuild would need it: *the chain-pose law probe, born and extended 2026-10-28 — recomputes ALL FIVE of the recorded red law channels (1/2/2s from events, 3/3-info/4/3s from the snapshot pass, law 4 as two independent state-machine replicas) twice over the same battles, RIGID (`fl` undefined, exactly what `measure.mjs` does) vs CHAIN-AWARE (`fl` = the event's stored `aFl`/`bFl`/`fFl`), with law 1s and law 3s's body/rim/pair counts as chain-independent controls, a per-event chain attribution column, Part D's direct pose-error measurement, Part E's proof of the entry-pose stash route (E1 the entry record vs the live pre-tick state field by field, E2 the stash's staleness bound), Part F's replication of the renderer's edge-leading flip sweep (`from·cos(π·p)` over `CFG.flipMs`, sampled at each tick's entry pose) which is what proves law 3's mirror class is never drawn, the per-occurrence mirror-class classifier, a vacuity warning when law 3s has no chain-foe coverage, and a surgical check that no non-chain verdict moves; ~4 min for 20 naturals + the 5 forced cells. It attributed the 2026-10-27 red set, and its standing instruction was: run it before any `measure.mjs` law run, or the chain blindness re-reports phantom hits.*

**Standing baseline:** the standing fingerprint baseline is **`.qa/fp-shotgrow-r33.json`** (adopted 2026-10-31, when `shotgrow-r33` changed behaviour on purpose and moved exactly 1 of 8 — the staff+hammer s1 cell — leaving the other seven bit-identical; md5 `daa7971ce5d5693feb16fd0100910684`). Its predecessor `.qa/fp-stall-r25.json` (the stall pass; every landing from `lengrow-r26` through `review-r32` fingerprinted byte-identical to it — `review-r32` was the seventh consecutive 0/8) is **historical only**, as is the earlier `.qa/fp-lead-r22.json`: it recorded the facing pass, where the dagger and staff flagged `lead: -1` and a new baseline was written because behaviour had deliberately changed; the stall pass then rewrote the baseline again and it held until `shotgrow-r33`. It held again at `nocombo-r34` (0 of 8 moved — the spear retirement is invisible to these cells by construction, and the neutrality claim for it rests on build-proof N/O instead). It MOVED 8 of 8 at `bindfix-r35` — sanctioned, because the bind fix touches every latched bind (nudge sign) and every latch (clinch floor), so no battle is untouched; the new standing baseline is **`.qa/fp-bindfix-r35.json`** (md5 `5db746efe53534ee8f3c1e1f51b0a11d`), and `fp-shotgrow-r33.json` joins the historical row. It moved **1 of 8** at `spingrow-r36` — sanctioned, exactly the hammer+dagger s5 cell whose dagger now snowballs, the other seven bit-identical (neither natural rolled a dagger); the new standing baseline is **`.qa/fp-spingrow-r36.json`** (md5 `8279c87272fde990d66516173f148bcc`), and `fp-bindfix-r35.json` joins the historical row. It moved **2 of 8** at `returngrow-r37` — sanctioned, and for the first time a NATURAL cell moved: the forced boomerang+shuriken s1 cell and natural s7, which rolls the boomerang (verified; 17,346 → 15,002 ticks — the Return shortens its holder's rounds), the other six bit-identical (`pow(x,0)=1`, ×1 mults exact, spread-null adds no keys); the new standing baseline is **`.qa/fp-returngrow-r37.json`** (md5 `2317ddeb32af6fdd220ccd2d973d220d`), and `fp-spingrow-r36.json` joins the historical row. A fresh baseline file is written **only** when behaviour changes on purpose — never to make a regression disappear.

**Doc tools (2026-10-23 hygiene pass, owner pick):** `scratch/navgen.mjs` regenerates this file's navigation block in place (parts table + Measured-facts entry index, iterated to a fixed point so its quoted line numbers are true of the file it writes; `--check` verifies without writing) and `scratch/docreflow.mjs` splits the record's 4–13 KB single-line paragraphs (whitespace-only, refused unless the whole-file non-whitespace byte stream, the word stream, every span's `**`/backtick parity and the entry-heading count are all preserved; `--check` is the idempotency proof). Run both after any edit that moves lines — they are the reason this file stays navigable at a quarter-megabyte of dense record. **Baseline tools (added 2026-10-28):** `scratch/measure-agg.mjs` sums the split-run part files into one aggregate and asserts the parts share no cell; `scratch/measure-summary.mjs` derives the per-weapon, per-class and shield tables from them, and with `--csv` writes two distillates — `scratch/baseline-484.csv` (one row per cell: rounds, winner split, Speedy%, median round sec, shots thrown/landed/swatted, source part) and `scratch/baseline-laws.csv` (one row per part: the law counters and integrity fields). Both were cross-checked against this record and reproduce all 14 published totals exactly. The six raw `scratch/m-0*.txt` part files were RETIRED from the workspace later the same day (owner go — he holds local copies, and their md5s are recorded above): the CSVs carry the full 484-row cell table and the headline law counters, but the complete per-part law-block text now exists only in his local copies, and regenerating them is 7.16 hours across two machines. The owner's 3-seed calibration capture was retired in the same cleanup — its md5 and its conclusion are the record, the artifact is not.

Tier guide: `--solidity` — **the owner's tunneling/overlap suite** (owner request 2026-10-19; the 12-cell/76-battle version measured **7m18s** at 2 sandbox jobs, ~2–3 min on a 6–8-core machine — inside the 10-min target; grown to 23 cells at the 2026-10-22/23 roster changes (batch-4 + javelin + tome + scroll + orb) — ≈109 battles, ≈11 min at 2 sandbox jobs / **≈3–4 min on a 6–8-core machine — the ~10-min target is the OWNER'S-MACHINE line and is met; retargeted 2026-10-23 at owner pick**, so no coverage was cut to chase a sandbox number. The sandbox cost is core count, not inefficiency: one `loadSim` per worker, an atomic work-stealing cursor, `jobs = availableParallelism()`, and the wall clock dominated by the few battles that exhaust `BUDGET_SEC` 420 (a capped battle ≈7× a typical one; the cap class is ~10 of the 23 cells plus the cap-prone naturals). The rejected levers, kept here so the choice is not re-litigated blind: a reduced budget for known-capped cells (≈5 min, risk — a late-battle law break would hide until the sanctioned full run) · naturals 40 → 10 in the fast tier (≈27% off) · a seed split, fast cells 1 / cap-prone 3 (≈20% off). Re-measure at the next sanctioned run): naturals + the curated 23-cell overlap gauntlet × 3 seeds (wide-head pin fights, the tiny-MINR weapons that stress the substep budget — *corrected by measurement 2026-10-28: the roster min is the scroll's 1.60, then axe 1.80, wand/flail/knives 2.00; the scythe is 2.40 and the orb is the outlier at 21.80, so the earlier "scythe's 1.9 is the roster min" text predated the length band* — mixed-speed shot pairs including the true worst pair wand+crossbow (spark 800 + bolt 860), ricochet/curve paths, and the chain class: flail+hammer, nunchaku mirror, nunchaku+scythe); every law is still audited, laws 3/3s/4s/5s are the point; the gauntlet lives in `SOLIDITY_CELLS` inside measure.mjs and must be updated when the roster changes · `--quick` (naturals + determinism, ~3 min at 2 jobs) after any rule change · `--vs <weapon>` (naturals + that weapon's 2N−1 cells) after a single-weapon merge · `--cells a+b,…` surgical · default/`--full` (the 484-cell matrix at 22 weapons) for balance sessions and anything touching contact/resolver/laws · `--matrix-only` splits a full baseline into disjoint chunks whose law counters sum exactly.

- **`measure.mjs`** loops the pure sim headlessly: termination, determinism, winner spread, rounds distribution, battle/round length vs the 1min+ target, combat-stall gaps (counts hit/parry/swat/bounce), event rate, the ordered-combo win matrix (**forced**, 484 cells × 8 shared seeds via the curator knob at the 22-weapon roster, plus the natural-roll matrix), and ridiculousness (max spin/damage, buzzsaw rate). The **Core contact law audit** (all six rules, re-verified from the exact stored poses on stride-13 snapshots) and the **seven shot-law checks** (1s–7s, listed in *Projectile weapons*) are built in. Known expected reds: termination (shield+shield) and the 300s line (projectile mirrors) — annotated in its output. *Amended 2026-10-28: no longer "until the balance pass" — the owner ruled the round-ending stalls DELIBERATE until the skills pass (no round timer, no escalation ramp, no shield fix), so at the sanctioned run these are expected reds by design, not debt to clear first.* Updated to v1.x reality at v1.2 (launch doctrine in 6s, projectile rule in 7s and the law-4 replica, worst-pair budget note in 5s), rebuilt the same day with parallel workers + run tiers (report proven byte-identical at any job count), first-run twice (part-0 naturals + the `--solidity` gauntlet — both recorded in **Measured facts**), and trimmed of the dormant parts-class law-3 counters/lines (one representation class since v0.9). The forced-matrix portion is deferred to the sanctioned run on the owner's machine (*amended 2026-10-28 — this read "deferred to the balance pass", which has since landed*).
- **`.qa/projectile-fix.mjs`** (born 2026-10-19, **RETIRED 2026-10-28** in the pre-skills cleanup, owner go) — was the fast targeted probe for the projectile rule, launch doctrine and budget guarantee; six forced cells × 3 seeds (~2–3 min since CIRCLES_V2; ~36s at birth), plus the termination red as information, and the iteration tool while `measure.mjs` stayed gated. **Consequence worth stating plainly: there is no longer a fast targeted check for changes touching shots, holds or the damage gate. What remains in that role is `chromecheck.mjs` (~4s, render/clock guards) and `.qa/inscribe.mjs` (~6s, the permanent inscription gate), with `measure.mjs` still owner-gated. If the skills pass starts moving shot behaviour, rebuilding this probe is the cheap fix.**
- **`.qa/pair-proof.mjs`** (**added 2026-10-28, `pair-r31`** — the pairing step's probe, Tier 0, ~25s, read-only). It exists because the standing fingerprint suite **cannot** cover this mechanism: fingerprints play `DEFAULT_CAST`, where `f.slot === f.idx`, so a slot/idx mix-up is invisible to them by construction. It runs pairings where they differ (`[1, 0]` and the mirror `[0, 0]`), sweeps the foe invariant `fs[f.slot] === f && fs[1 - f.slot] !== f` every tick, checks that every event actor ref is a slot with `to === 1 - by` and that `st.o` indexes `fs`, and **asserts its own sample sizes** so a future stall fails loudly instead of passing on zero observations (the house vacuity rule). Its decisive check isolates the actor's skill from the weapon's trait by forcing mirror hammers: damage-per-hit must equal `trait.dmg + skill.dmg` exactly and **exchange sides** under the swap. **Section I (added `review-r32`) drives the RENDERER**: it loads block 1 against a *recording* canvas stub, captures `requestAnimationFrame` and hand-steps the real frame loop through the reveal, then asserts the poster's `fillText` names and skill prose follow `G.cast` — plus a comment-aware static tripwire over every `COLS`/`TINTS`/`FIGHTERS`/`SKILL_SHORT` index in the file, requiring each to be an identity (`LEN_CAP` left the audited list at `nocombo-r34` with the table itself; the audit now counts 27 reads). That section was written because a real slot-keyed-colour bug shipped in `pair-r31` past sections A–H, the fingerprints and chromecheck alike; **it is proven to fire** (run against the pre-fix canonical it reports 4 FAILs and names all 13 offending sites). Read sections E, F and I after any edit touching `f.idx`, `f.slot`, `G.cast`, the event payloads, `SPAWN`, or any renderer read of a cast table.
- **`chromecheck.mjs`** runs *both* script blocks under DOM stubs, advances the real frame loop past the dice reveal, fires the poll loop and asserts the scorebug and the locked Columns legend actually render (names, score, rows, shorthand copy), that the fight clock (`G.phaseT`) really advances past the reveal, that the sim clock freezes once the battle is over, and that hitstop really freezes (`stopMs > 0` seen over the battle-to-end run, `G.t` never advancing during a freeze — guard added 2026-10-19). **Pairing section (added 2026-10-28, `pair-r31`):** it also drives `APP.setCast([1, 0])` and asserts the chrome reads the cast through `G.cast[slot]` — scorebug names and identity swatches exchange sides together, legend rows exchange, the slot-keyed score stays put, the winner overlay names the ACTOR, and `setCast(null)` restores the default. This is the only coverage of the chrome's pairing path; `pair-proof.mjs` loads the sim block alone and cannot see it. The K.O.-card guard (staged 1–1 tie, recorded `fillText` calls) was retired with the card itself at owner word 2026-10-21 ("i dont want it to be announced"). The chrome phase line was removed at owner word 2026-10-19; the two guards that used to read it moved to these state/canvas surfaces. It exists because chrome-only breakage twice reached the owner's eyes before any harness saw it. Note: its stub battle rolls a natural seed — on the rare shield+shield or long mirror roll it can time out; that is the balance red, not chrome breakage (re-run to disambiguate).
- **`ingest.mjs`** — **DORMANT since roster-r12 (2026-10-25, close item 2a): the artist-kit route superseded the SVG gate; retained should supplier SVGs ever arrive, with no privilege.** Was: the weapon merge gate: validate → normalize → cover → combat gauntlet on a temp copy → merge only on PASS. Every **SVG** candidate enters through it — supplier art gets no privilege. Without `--into` it is a dry run; its report lines are GAMES.md record material. Caveats at v1.2: the merge template hardcodes the melee trait (launchers need a post-merge edit), its gauntlet now runs against 21 opponents at the 22-weapon roster, and the v1.0 batch bypassed it via the in-file builder (open question 7).
- **The preview's sim is pure JS** (`newGame`, `tick`) at the top of the `<script>`, no DOM until the renderer section; the harnesses extract the first `<script>`, stub `document` / `window` / `performance` / `requestAnimationFrame` and the canvas context (a `Proxy` returning no-op-ables works), strip the IIFE wrapper, loop `tick()`. Keep one harness per preview or reuse.
- **Run chromecheck (+ `inscribe.mjs` for anything touching contact or weapon art) after every change that touches the preview** — the `projectile-fix` probe this rule named was retired 2026-10-28; `measure.mjs` when the owner says go. Record resulting facts in **Measured facts** with the build they came from. The owner never runs the harnesses — they are the assistant's instrument: reproduce any playtest symptom headlessly (same seed) before patching. A test suite no human runs by hand is normal; this one's CI is the assistant.

## 2026-11-11 — #734 · A `.gitattributes` PINS LF ON CHECKOUT, SO THE WINDOWS CRLF MD5 TRAP IS GONE — THE CANONICAL IS UNTOUCHED (docs-cleanup session 2)

- **THE ORDER.** Session 2's brief listed six open items and recommended `.gitattributes` first; the owner answered *"do it"* to that recommendation. Nothing else on the list was started.
- **WHAT CHANGED.** One new tracked file, `.gitattributes`, three rules: `*.html`, `*.mjs` and `*.md` are `text eol=lf`. It changes **checkout** behaviour only. Every committed blob was already LF (measured: 0 of the tracked files contain a CR), so no blob, no md5 of a committed file and no canonical byte moves. The canonical stays **5,392 lines**, stamp `links-r87`, md5 **`99bb910d74d7390a435ce3354fb9320f`** (LF, the committed bytes). `.json` is not covered: no check on it depends on line endings.
- **WHY.** Git for Windows defaults to `core.autocrlf=true`, so the owner's working tree holds CRLF and `md5sum` on it gives **`e4452e0925a9eed4d899e7c053c8053a`** — the same file, not a change. `eol=lf` on a path overrides `core.autocrlf`, so after a re-checkout the plain `md5sum` on Windows equals the committed-blob md5.
- **MEASURED, IN A LINUX SANDBOX THAT EMULATES WINDOWS (`core.autocrlf=true`; NOT the owner's machine).** Before: the working html md5 was `e4452e09…`. After fast-forwarding this change in: **unchanged** — merging `.gitattributes` does **not** rewrite files already on disk, and a plain `git checkout -- <file>` is a no-op because git sees the file as clean. Two ways did renormalize it, each giving `99bb910d…` with a clean `git status`: **(a)** delete the file, then `git checkout -- <file>`; **(b)** `git rm --cached -r . && git reset --hard` (this discards uncommitted work, so commit or stash first). After (b) the working html and every `.md` had 0 CRs.
- **WHAT HAS NOT BEEN RUN.** The battery was not run: no code and no canonical byte changed. `measure.mjs` did not run. The owner's real working tree has not been checked, and the push has not been done (the sandbox has no credentials). Open items 2–6 of the session-2 brief are untouched, including the ~68 KB of Measured facts, the battery runner and the remote branch `spinclash-item-three-triage-80aa6`.
